We therefore pair AIA with Reality-Referenced Texture Restoration: matched re-graining from each real frame's non-face regions, band-split transfer of sub-identity micro-texture from the real footage, and a second, spectral acceptance channel refereed by the footage's own spectrum. Identity-anchor density, we argue, is a controllable quality dial, and we specify falsifiable experiments -- drift-versus-gap curves, uniform-versus-adaptive placement at matched budgets, student training on AIA-minted data, and texture ablations with a human beauty-filter study -- that would validate or refute the proposal.

</details>


### [74] [DAPM: UAV Monocular Depth Estimation from Any Height, Pitch, Roll and FOV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21438)
*Tong Ling,Wenhui Diao,Yingchao Feng,Hanbo Bi,Zhongyan Hou,Xian Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DAPM，首个专为无人机航拍图像设计的单目深度估计框架，能联合估计相机姿态与深度，应对无人机动态视角变化的挑战。通过理想地面深度(IGD)模块和渐进量化区间(PQB)模块，在自建UAPD数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机在实际部署中相机姿态高度动态（高度、俯仰、横滚、视场角持续变化），现有单目深度估计方法难以泛化到这些多样视角及航拍场景中广泛的深度分布范围。现有方法缺乏针对无人机视角变化的专门设计。

Method: 1）通过理论分析建立无人机视角与视距的几何对应关系；2）提出DAPM框架，包含IGD模块（利用几何关系实现密集相机姿态监督并增强深度特征）和PQB模块（粗到细的渐进量化区间，实现渐进监督和层级量化，提升复杂航拍场景的鲁棒估计）。

Result: 在自建UAPD数据集（包含全面且连续的姿态参数分布）上，DAPM在深度估计和相机姿态估计指标上均达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: DAPM是首个专门针对无人机航拍视角的单目深度估计框架，通过几何先验引导的IGD和渐进量化的PQB模块，有效解决了无人机动态视角下的深度估计难题，推动了该领域的发展。

Abstract: Monocular depth estimation is a fundamental prerequisite for 3D reconstruction and autonomous navigation in Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs). In practical deployments, UAVs operate under highly dynamic camera poses characterized by continuous variations in height, pitch, roll, and field of view (FOV). Existing monocular depth estimation methods frequently fail to generalize across such diverse perspectives and the expansive scale of depth distributions inherent in aerial scenes. To address these challenges, we establish a quantitative representation of UAV viewing angles through rigorous theoretical analysis, deriving the geometric correspondence between viewing angles and view distances using the ground plane as a reference for observation. Building upon this, we propose Depth Estimation for Any Perspectives Model (DAPM), representing the first monocular framework specifically designed for UAV aerial imagery to jointly estimate camera pose and depth under continuously varying viewpoints. Specifically, we introduce an Ideal Ground Depth (IGD) module that leverages the derived geometric relationships between UAV perspectives and view distances to implement dense camera-pose supervision and enhance depth features. And we further develop a coarse-to-fine Progressive Quantization Bins (PQB) module. By incorporating progressive supervision and hierarchical quantization bins, the PQB module enables robust estimation in complex UAV aerial imagery. To evaluate the proposed framework, we present the UAV Any Perspectives Depth (UAPD) dataset, featuring comprehensive and continuous distributions of pose parameters. Experimental results on UAPD demonstrate that DAPM achieves state-of-the-art performance across both depth and camera-pose estimation metrics. The source code and datasets are available at: https://github.com/ThisIsLT/DAPM.

</details>


### [75] [GrainGS: Gradient-Decoupled Gaussian Splatting for Efficient Dynamic Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21448)
*Jiahao He,Yihua Shao,Zhengkai Zhao,Pan Gao,Fei Ma,Jingcai Guo,Hao Tang,Nicu Sebe,Qi Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GrainGS提出一种结合层级锚点骨架与逐高斯形变的动态场景重建框架，通过静态预热、梯度阻断、规范-残差外观分解等机制，在合成单目和真实多视角基准上实现了高质量重建、实时渲染（435.6 FPS）和紧凑存储（4.67 MB）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态3D高斯溅射方法中，逐基元方法灵活但易导致冗余生长，锚点基方法空间规整但抑制局部运动。GrainGS旨在兼顾细粒度运动建模、结构稳定性与紧凑表示。

Method: 1) 静态预热阶段建立时间不变的规范表示；2) 联合训练中用停止梯度操作阻断形变对规范位置的梯度路径，保留直接优化；3) 每个高斯独立预测位置、旋转、尺度的时变偏移；4) 规范-残差外观分解建模帧相关光度变化，避免将其强制纳入几何形变。

Result: 合成基准上平均PSNR达36.98 dB，渲染速度435.6 FPS，存储仅需4.67 MB，在高质量重建、实时新视角合成和紧凑存储方面均表现优异。

Conclusion: GrainGS通过层级锚点骨架与逐高斯形变的有机结合，有效平衡了动态场景重建中的运动灵活性、结构规整性和表示紧凑性。

Abstract: Dynamic scene reconstruction with 3D Gaussian Splatting requires a balance between fine-grained motion modeling, structural stability, and compact representation. Existing per-primitive methods provide flexible local deformation but often suffer from redundant primitive growth, while anchor-based methods improve spatial regularity at the cost of suppressing locally varying motion. To address these issues, we present GrainGS, a dynamic Gaussian framework that combines a hierarchical anchor scaffold with per-Gaussian deformation. A static warm-up stage first establishes a time-invariant canonical representation from observations across all timestamps. During joint training, a stop-gradient operation blocks the deformation-mediated gradient pathway to the canonical positions while preserving their direct refinement through the reconstruction objective. Each Gaussian then predicts independent temporal offsets for position, rotation, and scale, enabling detailed local motion within a structurally constrained scaffold. A canonical-residual appearance decomposition further models frame-dependent photometric changes without forcing them into geometric deformation. Experiments on synthetic monocular and real-world multiview benchmarks show that GrainGS achieves high reconstruction quality, real-time novel view synthesis, and compact storage. Under the synthetic benchmark setting, it reaches an average peak signal-to-noise ratio of 36.98 decibels, renders at 435.6 frames per second, and requires 4.67 megabytes of storage.

</details>


### [76] [SPDCN: Strip-based Deformable Convolutional Network for Steel Surface Defect Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21456)
*Zhongming Liu,Bingbing Jiang,Guangxin Wan,Xiang Zou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对钢表面缺陷分割中标准卷积各向同性感受野无法适配细长缺陷的问题，提出SPDCN网络：通过模糊增强多尺度上下文模块（FMCM）捕获多尺度信息，以及自适应方向感知可变形卷积（ADADC）使采样网格沿缺陷主方向各向异性对齐。在NEU-Seg上达到89.60% mIoU，仅3.54M参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有钢表面缺陷分割方法在处理裂纹、划痕等细长各向异性缺陷时效果不佳，因为标准卷积的感受野是各向同性的，且刚性采样网格无法适应不规则的缺陷边界。

Method: 提出SPDCN，包含两个核心模块：1) FMCM：采用分组多分支卷积结合直觉模糊通道注意力机制，自适应捕获不同尺度缺陷的多尺度上下文信息；2) ADADC：用解耦的水平/垂直条状卷积替代传统偏移预测器，使可变形卷积的采样网格能沿缺陷主方向进行各向异性对齐。

Result: 在公开钢表面缺陷数据集上超越现有方法，NEU-Seg上达到89.60% mIoU，参数量仅3.54M。

Conclusion: SPDCN通过各向异性的条状卷积设计有效解决了细长缺陷分割难题，以轻量级参数实现了SOTA性能。

Abstract: Steel surface defect segmentation is critical for industrial quality inspection, yet existing methods struggle with elongated, anisotropic defects such as cracks and scratches due to the isotropic receptive fields of standard convolutions and rigid sampling grids that cannot adapt to irregular defect boundaries. To address these limitations, we propose Strip-based Predictor for Deformable Convolutional Networks (SPDCN) with two key innovations. The \textbf{Fuzzy-enhanced Multi-scale Context Module (FMCM)} employs group-wise multi-branch convolutions with an intuitionistic fuzzy channel attention mechanism to adaptively capture multi-scale contextual information across varying defect sizes. The \textbf{Adaptive Direction-Aware Deformable Convolution (ADADC)} replaces the conventional offset predictor with decoupled horizontal and vertical strip convolutions, enabling the deformable sampling grid to anisotropically align with the principal orientation of elongated defects. Extensive experiments on public steel surface defect benchmarks demonstrate that SPDCN consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, achieving 89.60\% mIoU on NEU-Seg with only 3.54M parameters. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/DWlzm .

</details>


### [77] [CLUIE: Clustering-Aware Recurrent Propagation with Local Structural Compensation for Underwater Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21467)
*Kui Jiang,Zefan Feng,Laibin Chang,Yan Luo,Junjun Jiang,Xiaopeng Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CRWKV框架，通过聚类感知语义动态重排序（CSDR）和暗响应调制局部传播（DMLP），将固定扫描路径的RWKV改造为内容自适应的token轨迹，实现水下图像增强中的区域自适应恢复，在多个基准上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下图像因波长依赖性吸收、散射和反向散射导致颜色失真、对比度下降和细节丢失，且不同区域退化程度不同，需要区域自适应恢复。现有视觉RWKV模型虽高效建模长程依赖，但其预定义扫描顺序与内容无关，无法适应空间非均匀的恢复需求。

Method: 提出CRWKV框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 聚类感知语义动态重排序（CSDR），根据语义特征相似性对token分组，从簇间上下文关系推导动态遍历顺序，使WKV状态沿语义相关区域累积；(2) 暗响应调制局部传播（DMLP），通过深度卷积提取局部结构响应，并利用邻域感知伪暗响应图自适应调制传播强度，补偿局部连续性信息。

Result: 在多个水下图像增强基准数据集上，CRWKV取得了最先进的定量指标和更优的视觉质量。

Conclusion: 内容自适应的token轨迹设计能够有效解决区域异质退化问题，为水下图像增强提供了一种高效且效果优异的新方案。

Abstract: Underwater image enhancement remains challenging due to wavelength-dependent light absorption, scattering, and backscattering, which jointly cause color distortion, contrast degradation, and detail loss. Since these degradations vary with scene depth and imaging conditions, different regions within the same image often exhibit heterogeneous degradation patterns and thus require region-adaptive restoration. Although visual RWKV models offer an efficient linear-complexity solution for long-range dependency modeling, their predefined scanning orders are content-agnostic and therefore fail to adapt recurrent state propagation to spatially non-uniform restoration demands. To address this limitation, we propose a Clustering-aware RWKV framework, termed CRWKV, which reformulates the fixed recurrent propagation path of conventional RWKV into a content-adaptive token trajectory. Specifically, we introduce Clustering-aware Semantic Dynamic Reordering (CSDR), which groups tokens according to semantic feature similarity and derives a dynamic traversal order from inter-cluster contextual relations. This design enables WKV states to be accumulated along semantically correlated regions rather than fixed spatial or spectral orders. Since dynamic reordering may disrupt the local continuity of original spatial neighborhoods, we further propose Dark-response Modulated Local Propagation (DMLP), which extracts local structural responses via depth-wise convolution and adaptively modulates their propagation strength using a neighborhood-aware pseudo-dark response map. In this way, local structural cues are compensated before recurrent aggregation while preserving content-adaptive long-range modeling. Extensive experiments on multiple underwater image enhancement benchmarks demonstrate that CRWKV achieves state-of-the-art quantitative performance and superior visual quality.

</details>


### [78] [Future Rendering $\neq$ Future Surface: A Benchmark and Dataset for Dynamic Surface Reconstruction Beyond the Observed Window](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21471)
*Yukun Shi,Minglun Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FutureSurf是一个用于评估未来时刻表面重建的受控诊断基准，揭示了现有方法对未来几何预测的显著差距（2.0-6.6×），并证明未来渲染质量与未来表面精度统计上不相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态场景重建评估仅在观测时间窗口内进行，但AR、机器人等实际部署需要未来时刻的几何信息，缺乏标准基准来测量这一能力。

Method: 构建包含8个解析定义受控运动（含3个证伪控制）的数据集，要求方法在序列前75%训练，在后25%未来时刻评估；使用Chamfer距离评分，提供地面真值侧可恢复性预言器。

Result: DG-Mesh在未来可预测运动上有2.7-4.1×的差距；证伪控制有效；Deformable-3DGS也有2.0-6.6×差距；未来渲染质量与未来表面精度统计上不相关。

Conclusion: 未来表面重建是当前方法尚未解决的核心问题，未来误差集中于运动区域，需要专门针对未来几何预测的评估和方法设计。

Abstract: Dynamic-scene reconstruction is almost always evaluated inside the observed time window, yet deployment settings such as AR overlays, robot interaction, and anticipatory planning need the future surface: the geometry at times beyond those captured. No standard benchmark measures this. We introduce FutureSurf, a controlled diagnostic benchmark and dataset for future-time surface reconstruction that trades scene diversity for exact future ground truth and falsification controls. A method trains on the observed first 75% of a sequence; we score its extracted per-frame surface on the held-out future by Chamfer distance, reporting absolute future CD as the primary score and the future/observed gap as a diagnostic. The dataset contains eight analytically defined controlled motions, including three falsification controls, with exact per-frame ground-truth meshes. We also provide a ground-truth-side recoverability oracle. The release includes split files, scoring code, a benchmark card, and Croissant metadata. On the controlled motions, the DG-Mesh backbone leaves a 2.7-4.1$\times$ gap even for futures predictable in principle (four of five recoverable from observed motion by a fixed rule), while the falsification controls behave as designed (the surface-invariant motion shows no gap). Beyond the contributed dataset, the gap persists across six animated DG-Mesh asset scenes and a second backbone, Deformable-3DGS (2.0-6.6$\times$; both share a deformation-MLP temporal model). The benchmark also shows that future rendering quality and future-surface accuracy are statistically decoupled, so the novel-view-synthesis metrics the field reports do not track future geometry. The future error is structured, concentrating where the surface moves. The dataset, evaluation toolkit, and scoring code are available on Hugging Face and GitHub (https://github.com/Ricky-S/futuresurf).

</details>


### [79] [Recurrent Sinusoidal INRs for Efficient High-Fidelity Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21485)
*Hyunmin Cho,Jaejun Yoo,Kyong Hwan Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了正弦循环（sinusoidal recurrence）作为隐式神经表示（INR）中谐波频谱富集的迭代机制。实验表明，该方法在图像和3D表示任务上以更少参数和优化步骤取得更高保真度，并良好迁移至超分辨率、NeRF和SDF任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐式神经表示（INR）在频谱建模能力上存在局限，尤其是如何通过迭代机制有效富集谐波频谱信息尚不明确。作者希望从频谱角度理解正弦激活与循环展开的结合机制，并据此设计更高效的架构。

Method: 1. 理论分析正弦激活如何诱导谐波线频谱，揭示循环展开（recurrent unrolling）如何扩展有效频谱支撑。2. 提出共享正弦块（shared sinusoidal block），通过迭代方式逐步精化隐式表示。3. 在图像和3D任务上对比前馈INR、非正弦循环变体及平衡态正弦模型。

Result: 在RGB图像基准上，该方法相比前馈基线以更少参数和更少优化步骤达到更高保真度；在超分辨率、NeRF和SDF任务上也展现出良好的迁移性能。

Conclusion: 正弦循环是一种有效的频谱富集机制，能够显著提升INR的表达效率，为设计更轻量、高效的隐式神经表示提供了新思路。

Abstract: We study sinusoidal recurrence as an iterative mechanism for harmonic spectral enrichment in implicit neural representations (INRs). Our analysis reveals that sinusoidal activations induce a harmonic line spectrum, providing a spectral account of how recurrent unrolling enriches the effective spectral support. We realize this principle with a shared sinusoidal block that iteratively refines the latent representation. We empirically validate the resulting spectral behavior against feed-forward INRs, non-sinusoidal recurrent variants, and equilibrium-style sinusoidal models. Complementing this analysis, we evaluate the proposed architecture across image and 3D representation tasks. On RGB image benchmarks, our method achieves higher fidelity than feed-forward baselines with fewer parameters and fewer optimization steps, and it further transfers favorably to super-resolution, NeRF, and SDF tasks.

</details>


### [80] [Texture++: Elevating 3D Asset Texture Resolution with a Region-Aware Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21504)
*Shuaiwei Wang,Shi Li,Jieting Xu,Yuchi Huo,Qi Wang,Wenting Zheng,Rengan Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Texture++提出一种在UV空间中通过多视角渲染和融合进行纹理超分辨率的新框架，利用自适应视角选择、四叉树纹理区域组织和扩散模型，显著提升低分辨率纹理的细节和连贯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大量3D资产因纹理分辨率低而被废弃，现有超分辨率模型忽略纹理贴图而专注于自然图像，需要一种高效且可泛化的纹理超分辨率模型来复兴影视和游戏等行业中的老旧资产。

Method: 1) 将UV空间的超分辨率任务重新形式化为多视角渲染并融合输出；2) 提出自适应视角选择策略以整合分散的UV纹理块；3) 引入四叉树纹理区域组织方法，从不同视角组合超分辨纹理并提供掩码区分需改进区域；4) 设计基于扩散的超分辨率模型，增强指定掩码区域的纹理分辨率并与周围区域无缝融合。

Result: 通过全面评估，Texture++生成的纹理在细节和连贯性上显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: Texture++框架有效解决了纹理超分辨率问题，能够将低分辨率纹理提升为高质量、高分辨率结果，复兴大量老旧3D资产。

Abstract: Numerous 3D assets are discarded due to low texture resolution, while current super-resolution models ignore texture maps and focus on natural images. An efficient and generalizable texture super-resolution model can revitalize a large corpus of aging yet valuable assets across industries such as film and video games. We present Texture++, a novel framework for texture super-resolution, which enhances the low-resolution textures of assets to produce high-resolution, high-quality results. Specifically, we reformulate the task of super-resolution in UV space into performing it across multiple rendered views and merging the outputs. Firstly, to achieve more complete and continuous textures in the view space, we propose an adaptive view selection strategy to integrate textures dispersed across UV texture patches. Furthermore, we introduce a quadtree-based texture region organization method for combining super-resolved textures from different viewpoints, providing masks to distinguish regions that require improvement. Finally, we design a diffusion-based super-resolution model that enhances the texture resolution for specified masked regions, seamlessly integrating with surrounding regions. Through comprehensive evaluations, we demonstrate that our approach yields textures with substantially improved detail and coherence over existing methods.

</details>


### [81] [Boosting Robustness for All-Weather Self-Supervised Depth Estimation in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21526)
*Mengshi Qi,Xiaoyang Bi,Xianlin Zhang,Huadong Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于多教师蒸馏和鲁棒雷达融合的自训练方法，通过不确定性感知多教师蒸馏和POV-BEV雷达融合技术，实现在恶劣天气条件下的自监督深度估计，在多种天气数据集上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恶劣天气条件下，传感器感知退化导致像素对应关系扭曲，自监督损失函数假设被违反，深度预测出现错误；同时，雷达点云在POV视角分布稀疏，难以进行有效的自监督融合。

Method: 1) **不确定性感知多教师蒸馏**：利用不同恶劣条件输入生成多样化教师模型，并通过不确定性建模加权知识蒸馏损失。2) **POV-BEV雷达融合**：利用相机像素射线约束建立POV与雷达BEV视角的关联，利用更稠密的雷达点云捕获互补视角信息。

Result: 在多种天气数据集上进行大量定性和定量实验，证明了所提方法的鲁棒性，取得了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 提出的自训练框架有效解决了恶劣天气下自监督深度估计中像素对应失真和雷达稀疏融合两大挑战，代码和模型已开源。

Abstract: Self-supervised depth estimation is challenging for safe autonomous driving under various adverse weather conditions due to sensor perception degradation. These challenges arise from two main aspects. Firstly, adverse conditions can distort pixel correspondences and violate the assumptions embedded in the self-supervised loss function, leading to erroneous depth predictions. Secondly, while radar is a widely adopted sensor in adverse weather conditions, the sparse distribution of radar points in the Point of View (POV) poses challenges for self-supervised fusion. To address these issues, we introduce a novel self-training pipeline using unpaired real all-weather data through multi-teacher distillation and robust radar fusion. We propose the Uncertainty-Aware Multi-Teacher Distillation method to generate diverse teacher models with different adverse condition inputs, and then employ uncertainty modeling to weigh the knowledge distillation loss. Additionally, we design the POV-BEV Radar Fusion approach, which leverages camera-pixel ray constraints to establish connections between the camera's Point of View (POV) and the radar's Bird's-Eye View (BEV). This approach enables the utilization of denser radar points, effectively capturing the complementary perspectives of both POV and BEV. Extensive quantitative and qualitative experiments demonstrate the robustness of our proposed method on all-weather datasets, achieving state-of-the-art performance. Our code and models are available at https://github.com/MICLAB-BUPT/RobustDepth.

</details>


### [82] [ElasticTTT: Prior-Preserving Test-Time Tuning for Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21529)
*Yueyi Liu,Chi Zhang,Sen Cui,Miao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ElasticTTT提出了一种解决扩散模型测试时微调（TTT）中"先验崩溃"问题的新框架，通过目标分布正则化、对比CFG和异步噪声调度三种技术，在保留生成先验的同时实现一次性视频编辑的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准TTT与扩散模型的分布映射本质存在根本性不匹配：生成模型旨在学习整个数据分布，而TTT是单点优化。这种不匹配导致"先验崩溃"——模型丢弃文本条件和空间潜变量，退化到生成源视频或混淆不同区域特征，严重限制了视频编辑的生成弹性和质量。

Method: 1) **目标分布正则化**（Target Distribution Regularization）：防止模型陷入尖锐的记忆极小值，保持生成多样性；2) **对比CFG**（Contrastive CFG）：引导推理过程远离源视频的偏差，增强对编辑指令的响应；3) **异步噪声调度**（Asynchronous Noise Schedule）：保护未编辑区域的完整性，避免不必要的修改。

Result: 在一次性视频编辑任务上达到SOTA性能，理论分析和大量实验均证明ElasticTTT成功保留了基础模型的生成先验。

Conclusion: 通过解决TTT与生成模型的根本性不匹配问题，ElasticTTT为视频编辑中的先验保持提供了有效方案，平衡了编辑能力与生成质量。

Abstract: Test-Time Tuning (TTT) on pretrained diffusion models has emerged as a powerful paradigm for video editing. However, there exists a foundational mismatch between the distribution-mapping nature of generative models and the single-point optimization of standard TTT. In this paper, we demonstrate that this mismatch triggers \textit{Prior Collapse}, a degenerate state where the model discards the text conditions and spatial latents, collapsing generations to the source video, or entangling the features of distinct regions. To resolve this, we propose \textbf{ElasticTTT}, a novel framework that preserves the prior generative distribution and rescues generative elasticity. Specifically, we propose \textit{Target Distribution Regularization} to prevent sharp memorization minima, \textit{Contrastive CFG} to guide inference away from source biases, and \textit{Asynchronous Noise Schedule} to preserve unedited regions. Extensive evaluations, supported by theoretical analysis, demonstrate that ElasticTTT successfully preserves the generative prior of the base model, achieving state-of-the-art performance on one-shot video editing.

</details>


### [83] [Towards Robust Iris Recognition Through Occlusion Identification and Conditional Diffusion-Based Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21545)
*Kamrul Hasan,Mylene C. Q. Farias,Oleg V. Komogortsev*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种遮挡感知的虹膜识别框架，包含遮挡类型识别、扩散模型重建和深度学习识别三个模块，在CASIA数据集上证明能有效提升遮挡情况下的识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有虹膜识别方法在虹膜被眼睑、睫毛等遮挡时性能下降，仅利用可见区域不足以应对大面积遮挡的情况。

Method: 提出三阶段框架：1）残差2D CNN识别遮挡类型；2）条件去噪扩散模型（基于遮挡图像、二值掩码和预测的遮挡类型）重建被遮挡区域；3）改进的VGG19-HPMNet（VGG19+水平金字塔映射）提取全局和局部特征进行识别。

Result: 在CASIA-Iris-Thousand数据集上，该框架通过识别遮挡类型、重建遮挡区域并重新评估恢复后的虹膜样本，显著提升了虹膜识别性能。

Conclusion: 遮挡类型识别+扩散模型重建+深度学习识别三阶段的协同框架能有效处理虹膜遮挡问题，为实际应用中虹膜识别鲁棒性提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Iris recognition is a reliable biometric approach that identifies individuals using the distinctive and stable texture of the iris. However, recognition performance can degrade when discriminative iris texture is partially occluded by eyelids, eyelashes, specular reflections, or other acquisition artifacts. Existing approaches often perform recognition directly on degraded samples or rely only on the remaining visible iris region, which may be inadequate when substantial texture is corrupted. To address this limitation, we propose an occlusion-aware iris recognition framework with three sequential modules: occlusion-type identification, diffusion-based reconstruction, and deep-learning-based recognition. First, a residual 2D CNN-based network determines whether an iris image is non-occluded or belongs to one of the controlled occlusion categories. Second, the occluded image, binary mask, and predicted occlusion type condition a denoising diffusion probabilistic model to reconstruct the corrupted region. Finally, VGG19-HPMNet, a modified VGG19 model with horizontal pyramid mapping, extracts discriminative global and part-wise local iris features for recognition. Experiments on the CASIA-Iris-Thousand dataset under a controlled synthetic-occlusion protocol show that the proposed framework improves iris recognition performance by identifying the occlusion type, reconstructing masked regions, and re-evaluating the restored iris samples.

</details>


### [84] [UnDA: Unpaired Domain Alignment for Cross-Modal Knowledge Transfer in Medical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21546)
*Rafsan Jany,Shadab Tanjeed Ahmad,Ahsan Bulbul,Tahsinul Islam,Md Azam Hossain,Abu Raihan Mostofa Kamal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UnDA框架，通过锚点引导的跨模态知识蒸馏，在不依赖配对数据的情况下实现异质模态间的结构知识迁移，在分割任务上提升目标模态精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态方法性能优于单模态，但获取配对的临床数据极具挑战；现有跨模态蒸馏方法难以应对大模态间隙和源域预测噪声传播问题。

Method: 1) 与骨干网络无关的对齐模块(Alignment Module)，通过注意力池化提取语义结构化类令牌；2) 不确定性加权最优传输(UCT-OT)，根据预测置信度动态加权特征级对齐；3) 每类ProtoNCE目标函数，维护稳定原型记忆以增强全局判别性。

Result: 在严格非配对设置下，代表性分割任务中目标模态的准确性和边界精度均获得一致提升。

Conclusion: 无需配对数据集，即可在异质数据源间有效迁移有意义的结构化知识。

Abstract: Multimodal based approaches often outperform single modality approaches in downstream tasks as the different modalities provide complementary information, yet acquiring paired clinical data remains a significant challenge in real world scenarios. While cross-modal knowledge distillation addresses this, existing methods often struggle with large modality gaps and the propagation of noise from uncertain source-domain predictions. To overcome these challenges, we propose UnDA, an anchor-guided framework for unpaired cross-modal distillation. Our approach introduces a backbone-agnostic Alignment Module that extracts semantically structured class tokens via an attention based pooling mechanism. To ensure robust knowledge transfer, we propose Uncertainty-Weighted Optimal Transport (UCT-OT), which dynamically weights feature-level alignment based on prediction confidence, effectively suppressing noisy supervision. Furthermore, a per-class ProtoNCE objective maintains stable prototype memories to enforce global discriminability across unpaired batches. Evaluations on representative segmentation tasks under strictly unpaired settings show consistent improvements in accuracy and boundary precision in the target modality, demonstrating that meaningful structural knowledge can be transferred across heterogeneous data sources without paired datasets.

</details>


### [85] [SANA-Video 2.0: Hybrid Linear Attention with Attention Residuals for Efficient Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21553)
*Junsong Chen,Jincheng Yu,Yitong Li,Shuchen Xue,Haozhe Liu,Jingyu Xin,Yuyang Zhao,Tian Ye,Zhangjie Wu,Zian Wang,Daquan Zhou,Ping Luo,Song Han,Enze Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SANA-Video 2.0 是一种混合视频扩散Transformer架构（5B/14B），通过Hybrid Linear-Softmax Attention（3:1比例）和Block Attention Residuals机制，在单GPU上实现720p视频生成，质量媲美全softmax模型但速度快3.2倍以上，VBench得分84.30（480p/13.2s），经Sol-Engine优化后5B模型720p/5s仅需13.06s，比Wan 2.2-A14B快120倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频扩散Transformer（DiTs）在全softmax注意力下计算复杂度呈二次增长，难以扩展到长序列、高分辨率视频生成；而纯线性注意力虽具有线性复杂度，但缺乏全秩token交互能力，表达力受限。

Method: 1）Hybrid Linear-Softmax Attention：以3:1比例混合门控线性注意力（O(N)复杂度）与周期性门控softmax锚点，恢复全秩交互；2）Block Attention Residuals (AttnRes)：将已完成块的摘要路由至后续线性层，实现锚点特征复用，提升深层有效秩约12%；3）从零训练完整混合架构，通过低分辨率代理研究确定25% softmax为最优质量-效率权衡点。

Result: 40步采样下480p VBench得分84.30（13.2s/单H100）；720p/60s编译前向传播比全softmax基线快3.2倍；Sol-Engine优化后5B模型720p/5s仅13.06s，比Wan 2.2-A14B快120倍。

Conclusion: 混合注意力设计以大幅降低的计算成本恢复了softmax级别的表达力，为可扩展的长视频、高分辨率生成提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We introduce SANA-Video 2.0, a hybrid video diffusion transformer instantiated at 5B and 14B scales under a unified architecture. Designed to generate high-quality video up to 720p on a single GPU, SANA-Video 2.0 matches full-softmax video DiTs in quality while retaining the favorable long-sequence scaling of linear attention. To avoid quadratic attention throughout, Hybrid Linear-Softmax Attention combines gated linear attention for O(N)-dominated mixing with periodic gated-softmax anchors at a 3:1 ratio, restoring the full-rank token interactions that pure linear attention lacks. To propagate these refreshed representations across depth, Block Attention Residuals (AttnRes) route completed block summaries into later linear layers, enabling anchor-feature reuse and boosting deep-layer effective rank by ~12%. Through from-scratch training, SANA-Video 2.0 learns the complete hybrid directly rather than linearizing pretrained models, with reduced-resolution proxy studies establishing 25% softmax as the optimal quality-efficiency trade-off. With 40-step sampling, SANA-Video 2.0 achieves a VBench score of 84.30 in 13.2s at 480p on a single H100, remaining competitive with far larger softmax video DiTs at a fraction of the latency. Its compiled DiT forward pass is 3.2x faster than a matched full-softmax baseline at 720p/60s, a gap that expands with video duration. Furthermore, full-stack Sol-Engine optimization (kernel fusion, caching, and sparse attention) accelerates this hardware-friendly backbone by a further 3.58x, bringing the 5B pipeline to 13.06s at 720p/5s and making it 120x faster than Wan 2.2-A14B on one H100. Overall, our hybrid design recovers softmax-level expressiveness at substantially reduced cost, unlocking scalable long, high resolution video generation.

</details>


### [86] [Visual Contrastive Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21556)
*Yijun Liang,Yunjie Tian,Yijiang Li,Yuqi Jia,Furong Huang,Tianyi Zhou,Di Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VCSD提出了一种无需外部教师、特权答案或视觉证据的纯输入条件驱动的在线自蒸馏方法，通过视觉对比学习提升多模态大模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线自蒸馏（OPSD）方法需要不对称信息（特权答案或视觉证据）来确保自教师提供比学生更强的学习信号。本文探究能否移除这些要求，实现仅靠输入条件驱动的更简洁的OPSD形式。

Method: 提出VCSD：在学生生成每个响应前缀时，EMA教师在同一提示和前缀下产生两个next-token分布——一个基于原始图像，另一个基于内容擦除的控制图像。通过二者token-wise log概率差突出由实例级视觉内容增加似然性的候选词，用此对比度在合理支撑集内锐化教师的原始图像分布，并将全分布目标蒸馏给学生。

Result: 在ViRL39K数据集上，VCSD在Qwen3-VL和Qwen3.5模型上持续优于匹配的OPSD方法。以Qwen3-VL为例，七个基准聚合从2B:62.27%→67.04%，4B:71.30%→73.16%，8B:72.51%→76.26%。

Conclusion: VCSD无需外部教师、特权答案、视觉证据信号、推理轨迹或额外推理成本，实现了更简洁高效的在线自蒸馏。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) is promising as it removes the external teacher required by on-policy distillation (OPD), yet it still needs asymmetric information between teacher and student to ensure that the self-teacher provides a stronger learning signal than the student. Existing methods create this asymmetry either through privileged answers or visual evidence. We ask whether both can be removed, yielding a simpler form of OPSD driven purely by input conditioning. For this purpose, we propose Visual Contrastive Self-Distillation, namely VCSD, which converts image-content removal into an on-policy self-distillation signal. At each student-generated response prefix, the EMA teacher produces two next-token distributions under the same prompt and prefix -- one conditioned on the original image and the other on a content-erased control. Their token-wise log-probability difference highlights candidates whose likelihood is specifically increased by the instance-level visual content. We use this contrast to sharpen the teacher's original-image distribution within its plausible support, and distill the resulting full-distribution target into the student. Using ViRL39K dataset, VCSD consistently outperforms matched OPSD across Qwen3-VL and Qwen3.5 models. For example, on Qwen3-VL, it improves the seven-benchmark aggregate from $62.27\% \rightarrow 67.04\%$ at 2B, $71.30\% \rightarrow 73.16\%$ at 4B, and $72.51\% \rightarrow 76.26\%$ at 8B. Furthermore, VCSD requires no external teacher, privileged answers, visual evidence signals, reasoning traces, or additional inference-time cost.

</details>


### [87] [Scene Parameter Saliency via Differentiable Light Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21562)
*Linas Beresna,Eugene Fiume*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"度量显著性图"概念，利用可微渲染器的反向传播梯度来分析场景参数对各类视觉度量指标的影响，发现不同度量产生截然不同的显著性排序，表明可微渲染生成的导数图像与原始图像同等重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统梯度显著性方法用于解释神经网络，但缺乏对物理渲染过程本身参数重要性的分析工具。作者希望借助可微渲染器，将梯度分析从神经网络权重扩展到物理场景参数，揭示不同视觉度量下场景元素的影响力差异。

Method: 利用可微渲染器对渲染图像上任意标量度量进行一次反向模式微分，得到每个场景参数的梯度（即度量显著性图）。方法无需修改渲染器，仅需定义度量函数并自动微分。

Result: 对心理物理眩光指标、平均场景亮度、神经感知评分等不同目标计算显著性图，发现同一场景下不同度量的参数重要性排序截然不同，某个目标的关键参数对另一目标可能毫无作用。

Conclusion: 可微渲染器产生的导数图像与原始渲染图像在场景理解上具有同等信息价值；显著性图是度量相关的，而非场景固有属性。

Abstract: Gradient-based saliency methods reveal which input features most influence a neural network's output, and are a standard tool for model interpretability. We observe that differentiable renderers, which are conventionally used for parameter optimisation, produce an analogous form of saliency: given any scalar metric evaluated on a rendered image, a single reverse-mode differentiation pass yields per-parameter gradients that identify which scene elements most influence the metric. We call these gradient fields metric saliency maps. Unlike neural saliency, which propagates attribution through learned weights, metric saliency propagates through the image formation process itself, including multi-bounce light transport, capturing parameter dependencies that are semi-opaque to manual inspection. We compute metric saliency maps for qualitatively different objectives: psychovisual glare indices, mean scene luminance, and neural perceptual scores. The saliency rankings differ substantially across metrics for the same scene, with parameters that dominate one objective being negligible for another. The saliency map is specific to the metric, not an intrinsic property of the scene. Our results suggest that differentiable renderers produce derivative images that are as informative for scene understanding as the primal images they were designed to generate.

</details>


### [88] [Self-Supervised Learning of Structured Dynamics from Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21576)
*Lukas Knobel,Andrew Zisserman,Yuki M. Asano*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出结构化动力学模型(SDM)，利用预训练图像ViT的冻结特征，通过未来特征预测显式分离相机运动与物体运动，结合自监督合成数据弱监督训练，在ProbeMotion评测集上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频中帧间变化由相机运动与物体运动两种动力学耦合而成，现有表示学习对此分解探索不足，而分离二者对学习鲁棒的运动表示至关重要。

Method: 提出SDM模型，基于预训练图像ViT的冻结特征，通过未来特征预测显式分离主要动力学来源（相机运动）与残差动力学（物体运动），结合真实视频自监督学习与合成Kubric数据的弱监督训练。

Result: 在ProbeMotion评测集上，SDM优于使用全局CLS或平均池化特征的基线，且在多个探针任务上与强监督方法VGGT相比不落下风，尽管监督信号弱得多。

Conclusion: 预训练图像模型可被有效改造为结构化视频动力学表示，为学习与分析潜在视频动力学提供了有用的归纳偏置。

Abstract: Understanding motion in video is a fundamental challenge for visual learning, as frame-to-frame change entangles two sources of dynamics: camera motion and object motion. This decomposition has remained underexplored in representation learning, partly because these factors are tightly coupled in natural videos and difficult to supervise separately. Yet recovering it is important for learning robust motion representations that separate meaningful object dynamics from camera-induced variation. We study whether such structured motion representations can be recovered from frozen features of a pretrained image vision transformer. We propose the Structured Dynamics Model (SDM), which explicitly separates the dominant source of temporal change from residual dynamics through future-feature prediction, rather than representing video change with a single entangled latent or with unstructured, spatially dense transition tokens. Training combines self-supervised learning on real video with weak supervision of scene dynamics on synthetic Kubric data. We evaluate SDM on ProbeMotion, a new evaluation suite spanning synthetic and real videos with camera motion, object motion, and combined dynamics. SDM outperforms backbone baselines using global CLS or average-pooled features, and compares favorably to strongly supervised representations such as VGGT on several probes, despite using substantially weaker supervision. These results suggest that pretrained image models can be readily repurposed into structured video-dynamics representations, providing a useful inductive bias for learning and analyzing latent video dynamics.

</details>


### [89] [Synthetic data generation framework for quality control automation in gravure printing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21577)
*Korota Arsène Coulibaly,Mohamed Hamlich,Khalid Hmali,Andrea Trombin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向凹版印刷质量控制的合成数据生成框架，自动生成高保真印刷缺陷图像及标注，用于训练目标检测模型RFDETR。在7533张合成图像上训练的模型在真实工业测试样本上达到80.9%的mAP，为零成本快速部署自动化缺陷检测提供了可行方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凹版印刷质量检测仍依赖人工目检，效率低、成本高、主观性强。深度学习模型虽有自动化潜力，但面临真实工业缺陷图像极度匮乏的瓶颈，难以训练出鲁棒的检测模型。

Method: 提出一种合成数据生成框架，可自动生成高保真度的凹版印刷缺陷图像（如折痕、条纹、套印不准等），并同时输出对应的边界框和标注信息。使用该框架生成7533张合成图像，用于训练SOTA目标检测模型RFDETR。

Result: 在合成数据上训练的RFDETR模型，在真实工业测试样本上取得了80.9%的mAP，验证了合成数据替代真实数据的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架为零成本、快速部署的印刷线缺陷自动化检测提供了可行方案，无需大规模人工数据采集，有助于推动印刷质量控制的自动化进程。

Abstract: Quality control in printing, particularly in rotogravure printing, still depends on slow, costly, and subjective manual inspection. Automated surface defect detection is critical for maintaining high-quality standards in rotogravure printing. Deep learning models give prospects for automation. However, training robust deep learning models, such as YOLO or Vision Transformers, is heavily hindered by the extreme scarcity of real-world industrial defects images. To overcome this limitation, this paper introduces a novel synthetic data generation framework tailored for rotogravure printing quality control. The proposed pipeline automatically generates high-fidelity images of specific printing defects (creases, streaks, misregistration, etc.) and outputs corresponding bounding boxes and annotations. To validate the framework, a synthetic dataset of 7533 images was generated and used to train the state-of-the-art object-detection model RFDETR. Experimental results demonstrate that the model trained on our synthetic data achieves a Mean Average Precision (mAP) of 80.9\% on real industrial testing samples. This framework provides a zero-cost, rapid-deployment solution for automating defect inspection in printing lines without requiring massive manual data collection.

</details>


### [90] [GraphVid: Interactive Graph-Controllable Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21580)
*Vedant Shah,Onkar Susladkar,Tushar Prakash,Kiet Nguyen,Tianjio Yu,Adheesh Juvekar,Muntasir Waheed,Ismini Lourentzou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GraphVid提出通过结构化交互图（interaction graph）实现多主体可控视频生成，并构建了GraphVid-Bench数据集，在更少数据和参数下显著优于Motion-I2V等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可控视频生成方法依赖文本提示或轨迹控制，难以精确描述多物体交互，且在遮挡、重叠场景下轨迹绘制困难、扩展性差。

Method: 提出GraphVid，一种以结构化交互图为条件的图像到视频生成模型；同时构建GraphVid-Bench大规模交互中心视频数据集，包含结构化关系标注，用于训练交互感知模型。

Result: 相比Motion-I2V，FID降低39.9%，FVD降低37.6%，PSNR从9.87提升至15.98，SSIM从0.38提升至0.61，且所需训练数据和参数更少。

Conclusion: 结构化语义接口（如交互图）是可控视频生成的一种有力范式，能实现灵活、精确的多主体控制。

Abstract: Controllable video generation remains challenging due to the difficulty of specifying precise multi-object interactions using text prompts or motion-control inputs that primarily constrain pixel movement. In practice, trajectory-based control often requires users to draw accurate tracks for multiple objects, which scales poorly with scene complexity and becomes ambiguous under occlusion or overlap. To enable flexible yet precise multi-subject control, we introduce $\textbf{GraphVid}$, a graph-conditioned image-to-video generation model that enables interactive control through structured interaction graphs. We further curate $\textbf{GraphVid-Bench}$, a large-scale interaction-centric video dataset with structured relational annotations to enable training of interaction-aware video generation models. Despite using substantially less training data and fewer trainable parameters than prior motion-control methods, GraphVid delivers strong controllability and video quality. Compared with Motion-I2V, GraphVid reduces FID by up to 39.9% and FVD by 37.6%, while improving PSNR (9.87=>15.98) and SSIM (0.38=>0.61). Our results highlight the potential of structured semantic interfaces as a powerful paradigm for controllable video generation.

</details>


### [91] [Inference-Time Scaling of Diffusion Models via Progressive Seed Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21591)
*Rogerio Guimaraes,Pietro Perona*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出渐进式种子剪枝(PSP)方法，通过在推理阶段前端加载探索、评估大量种子并积极剪枝，在固定计算预算下比最佳N采样、重要性采样和树搜索基线方法获得更高的GenEval分数和人类评估对齐度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散和流匹配模型在条件图像生成中占主导地位，但推理时扩展策略远不如自回归语言模型成熟。由于最终质量对初始噪声种子高度敏感，现有方法通常保持恒定内存占用进行种子搜索或重采样，忽略了推理时内存约束可放松的特性。

Method: PSP方法在推理阶段前端进行探索性计算：评估大量种子候选，对中间去噪估计进行评分，然后逐步剪枝候选集，只保留有潜力的轨迹完成完整去噪过程，同时保持模型评估总数固定。

Result: 在扩散和流匹配骨干网络上，PSP在奖励引导选择任务上持续提升效果。在GenEval自动评估和提示对齐人工评估上，均优于最佳N采样、重要性采样和树搜索基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过放松推理阶段恒定内存占用约束，前端加载探索结合积极剪枝策略是一种有效且未被充分探索的推理时扩展方向，能在固定计算预算下显著提升生成质量。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow-matching models dominate conditional image generation, yet inference-time scaling for these models is far less developed than for autoregressive language models. Because final quality is highly sensitive to the initial noise seed, many approaches spend extra compute on seed search or resampling under a black-box reward, but typically maintaining a constant memory footprint throughout inference. We show that relaxing this constraint enables an underexplored inference-time scaling axis: by front-loading exploration, evaluating many seeds early, and pruning aggressively, we can use a fixed compute budget more effectively. \emph{Progressive Seed Pruning} (\PSP) scores intermediate denoised estimates and progressively narrows the candidate set so that only promising trajectories are fully denoised, while keeping the total number of model evaluations fixed. Across diffusion and flow-matching backbones, \PSP \ consistently improves reward-guided selection and achieves higher GenEval scores (automated) and better human evaluation on prompt-alignment than best-of-$N$, importance-sampling, and tree-search baselines at matched compute. Project page: https://www.vision.caltech.edu/psp. Code: https://github.com/rogerioagjr/psp.

</details>


### [92] [Unified Video Dense Prediction from Disjoint Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21592)
*Yihong Sun,Seoung Wug Oh,Jiahui Huang,Bharath Hariharan,Joon-Young Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniD是一个统一的视频模型，利用预训练扩散模型的视觉先验，通过轻量级任务投影器和蒸馏步骤，从不相交的特定领域数据集中联合学习8种密集场景属性（深度、法线、语义分割、边界、人体部位、反照率、阴影、材质），无需标注重叠或伪标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有场景理解任务各自有独立标注，数据集之间不兼容，而当前统一系统要么依赖完全共标注数据，要么使用计算量大的伪标签方法，缺乏高效且通用的统一方案。

Method: 提出UniD统一视频模型，采用蒸馏策略：每个任务的专家模型通过轻量级任务投影器监督统一骨干网络，利用预训练扩散模型的强视觉先验弥合不相交训练数据间的领域差距。

Result: 与单任务专家和多任务基线相比性能具有竞争力，对分布外场景有强泛化能力，时序和跨任务一致性得到提升。

Conclusion: 证明了利用预训练扩散模型的视觉先验，通过蒸馏方法可以有效实现从不相交数据集中联合学习多种密集场景属性，无需标注重叠或伪标签，为统一场景理解提供了新范式。

Abstract: Scene understanding requires simultaneous prediction about geometry, appearance, and semantics. However, existing task-specific annotations are fragmented across incompatible, domain-specific datasets. Current unified systems circumvent this by restricting training to fully co-annotated data, or by incurring the large computational cost of pseudo-labeling. To mitigate this, we introduce UniD, a unified video model that jointly predicts eight dense scene properties-depth, surface normals, semantic segmentation, boundaries, human parts, albedo, shading, and materials-all learned from disjoint, domain-specific datasets. We propose a simple yet effective distillation step in which per-task experts supervise a unified backbone through lightweight task projectors, eliminating the need for annotation overlap or pseudo-labeling. Our key insight is that the strong visual priors of a pretrained diffusion model are sufficient to bridge the domain gaps introduced by disjoint training sources, enabling robust generalization to scene-task combinations never seen during training. UniD achieves competitive performance against per-task specialists and multi-task baselines, with strong generalization to out-of-distribution scenarios and enhanced temporal and cross-task consistency. Code and video results are available at https://unid-video.github.io/.

</details>


### [93] [Streaming Multi-Agent Autoregressive Diffusion Model with World State Registers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21594)
*Sicheng Mo,Yuheng Li,Ziyang Leng,Krishna Kumar Singh,Bolei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WorldWeaver提出了一种多智能体视频扩散模型，通过可学习的世界状态寄存器（world state registers）在智能体间共享和更新世界信息，并采用Mixture-of-Transformers架构分别建模世界状态和视觉帧，在双智能体Minecraft视频生成中提升了逻辑一致性和生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归视频扩散模型仅将历史观察作为条件上下文，在多智能体、多视角场景下难以维护共享的世界状态（例如跨智能体持久化、跨视图演化）。

Method: 1) 引入跨智能体世界状态寄存器（可学习token），在生成每个片段后动态更新；2) 使用多粒度监督信号（智能体状态、全局鸟瞰图、场景文本）来ground这些寄存器；3) 采用Mixture-of-Transformers架构，将世界状态建模和视觉帧建模的权重分离。

Result: 在双智能体Minecraft视频生成任务中，显式的世界状态建模显著提升了逻辑一致性和生成质量。

Conclusion: 显式的世界状态建模是解决多智能体视频生成中状态共享与一致性问题的有效途径。

Abstract: Multi-agent interactive world models should not only generate consistent observations, but also maintain world states that persist across agents and evolve across views. Existing autoregressive video diffusion pipelines carry forward observation history as conditioning context, which makes shared state difficult to maintain in multi-agent and multi-view settings. We present WorldWeaver (W^2), a streaming multi-agent video diffusion model that augments rollout with cross-agent world state registers: learnable tokens that store shared world information, track individual agent status, and are dynamically updated after each generated chunk. We ground these registers with supervision signals spanning individual agent status, global state views including bird's-eye views, and scene text. We further improve the architecture with a Mixture-of-Transformers design that uses separate weights for world state modeling and visual frame modeling. Extensive experiments in two-agent Minecraft video generation show that explicit world-state modeling improves logical consistency and generation quality.

</details>


### [94] [3D-Aware VLMs with Implicit and Explicit Geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21595)
*Wenhao Li,Xueying Jiang,Quanhao Qian,Deli Zhao,Ran Xu,Shijian Lu,Gongjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VLM-IE3D是一个统一框架，通过从RGB视频中学习隐式和显式3D几何表征来增强视觉语言模型的3D空间感知能力，在多个3D任务上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有2D视觉语言模型在处理需要细粒度空间理解的3D任务时表现不佳，缺乏有效的3D空间感知能力。

Method: 提出隐式几何令牌（IGTs）捕获高层几何先验，显式几何令牌（EGTs）编码重建的3D属性细节，并通过3D感知适配器将两种几何表征与2D视觉线索融合，无需额外3D输入。

Result: 在3D视频检测、3D视觉定位、3D密集描述和空间推理等多个3D任务上均取得一致领先性能。

Conclusion: 该RGB-only设计成功注入了强3D归纳偏置，有效弥合了2D VLM与3D任务之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Despite rapid progress, most existing vision-language models (VLMs) built from 2D visual inputs often struggle when handling various 3D tasks that require fine-grained spatial understanding and reasoning. To bridge this gap, we present VLM-IE3D, a unified framework that enhances the 3D spatial awareness of VLMs by equipping them with both implicit and explicit 3D geometries learned from RGB videos. Our VLM-IE3D introduces Implicit Geometry Tokens (IGTs) that capture high-level geometric priors from input videos, as well as complementary Explicit Geometry Tokens (EGTs) that encode detailed geometric structures from reconstructed 3D attributes. On top of that, VLM-IE3D comes with a 3D-aware adapter that effectively fuses the two types of geometric representations with 2D visual cues. This RGB-only design injects strong 3D inductive biases for fine-grained spatial understanding and reasoning without requiring any additional 3D inputs. Extensive experiments show that VLM-IE3D achieves superior performance consistently across various 3D tasks including 3D video detection, 3D visual grounding, 3D dense captioning, and spatial reasoning. Code and models are available at https://github.com/Vegetebird/VLM-IE3D.

</details>


### [95] [Deblurring in the Wild: A Real-World Image Deblurring Dataset from Smartphone High-Speed Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.19445)
*Syed Mumtahin Mahmud,Mahdi Mohd Hossain Noki,Prothito Shovon Majumder,Abdul Mohaimen Al Radi,Sudipto Das Sukanto,Afia Lubaina,Md. Mosaddek Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于智能手机慢动作视频构建的大规模真实世界图像去模糊数据集，包含42,000多对高分辨率模糊-清晰图像，比现有数据集大10倍，场景多样性高8倍，并发现现有SOTA模型在此基准上性能显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像去模糊数据集规模小、场景单一，难以训练出鲁棒且泛化能力强的去模糊模型，亟需一个大规模、多样化的真实世界基准数据集。

Method: 利用智能手机慢动作视频（每秒240帧）模拟长时间曝光模糊：通过平均多帧生成模糊图像，以时间中心帧作为清晰参考，构建了超过42,000对高分辨率模糊-清晰图像，涵盖室内外多种场景和运动类型。

Result: 数据集规模是现有常用数据集的约10倍，场景数量是8倍；多个SOTA去模糊模型在该基准上性能显著下降，表明该数据集具有更高的复杂性和挑战性。

Conclusion: 该数据集可作为更具挑战性的去模糊新基准，有助于推动鲁棒且泛化能力强的去模糊模型的发展。

Abstract: We introduce the largest real-world image deblurring dataset constructed from smartphone slow-motion videos. Using 240 frames captured over one second, we simulate realistic long-exposure blur by averaging frames to produce blurry images, while using the temporally centered frame as the sharp reference. Our dataset contains over 42,000 high-resolution blur-sharp image pairs, making it approximately 10 times larger than widely used datasets, with 8 times the amount of different scenes, including indoor and outdoor environments, with varying object and camera motions. We benchmark multiple state-of-the-art (SOTA) deblurring models on our dataset and observe significant performance degradation, highlighting the complexity and diversity of our benchmark. Our dataset serves as a challenging new benchmark to facilitate robust and generalizable deblurring models.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [96] [What is Good? Extracting and Testing Implicit Theories of Literary Quality from LLM Reasoning Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20425)
*Birger Moëll*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过两项研究探讨了具备推理能力的大语言模型如何评估文学质量。研究一构建了涵盖六个质量等级的30篇真实文本基准，发现DeepSeek模型达到79.3%的平均分类准确率，其推理轨迹显示模型更重视意图性（匠心、深度、独特声音）而非正确性。研究二对五篇经典散文进行六种系统退化操作，发现词汇简化对质量影响最小（0.41分），而结构和声音损失最为严重（2.78和2.34分），联合退化效果显著但呈次加性。研究表明LLM对写作质量的判断是整体性的、作者特异性的，且对结构特征比词汇特征更敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文学质量的评判标准在文学研究和计算语言学中一直是个悬而未决的问题。本文试图探究具备推理能力的大语言模型如何理解和评估文学作品的写作质量，以揭示其隐含的质量理论，并为自动化写作反馈和计算美学提供参考。

Method: 采用两项互补性研究。研究一构建了从经典文学到匿名论坛帖子的六个质量层级、30篇真实文本基准，让DeepSeek模型进行分类并提取其推理轨迹中的质量理论，同时通过风格匹配但不可识别的段落进行熟悉度实验。研究二对五篇经典散文进行六种系统退化操作（词汇简化、节奏扁平化、意象移除、声音泛化、结构简化及联合退化），评估各版本的质量得分变化。

Result: 研究一：模型达到79.3%的层级分类准确率；推理轨迹显示模型重视意图性而非正确性；源文本识别可能虚高评分。研究二：词汇简化造成最小质量损失（0.41±0.46分），结构简化（2.78分）和声音泛化（2.34分）损失最大；联合退化效果-5.64分且呈次加性；与Qwen QwQ的对比显示出相同的定性模式。

Conclusion: LLM对写作质量的判断是整体性的、作者特异性的，更关注结构特征而非词汇特征。这对自动化写作反馈和计算美学具有重要启示。

Abstract: What makes writing "good" remains a persistent question in literary studies and computational linguistics. We present a two-study investigation of how reasoning-enabled LLMs evaluate literary quality.
  In Study 1, we construct a benchmark of 30 real texts spanning six quality tiers, from canonical literature to anonymous forum posts, and extract the model's implicit theory of quality from its reasoning traces. Across five DeepSeek replications, the model achieves 79.3% mean tier-classification accuracy. The traces reveal a consistent stated theory: the model values intentionality over correctness, prioritizing craft, depth, and distinctive voice. A familiarity experiment with style-matched but unrecognizable passages suggests that source recognition may inflate scores, although this is confounded by genuine quality differences between canonical originals and researcher-written pastiches.
  In Study 2, we probe this theory through systematic degradation of five canonical prose passages. We apply six manipulations - vocabulary simplification, rhythm flattening, imagery removal, voice genericization, structure simplification, and combined degradation - and reevaluate each version. Vocabulary simplification causes the smallest quality loss (0.41 +/- 0.46 points), far below structure (2.78) or voice (2.34) loss. Combined degradation is devastating (-5.64) but subadditive. An exploratory comparison with Qwen QwQ shows the same broad qualitative pattern.
  Together, these studies suggest that LLM judgments of writing quality are holistic, author-specific, and more sensitive to structural than lexical features, with implications for automated writing feedback and computational aesthetics.

</details>


### [97] [Knowledge Injection Exists in MoE? Exploring Expert-Aware Contrast Decoding in MoE for Mitigating LLMs'Hallucinations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20426)
*Xinyue Fang,Zhiliang Tian,Zhen Huang,Ziyi Pan,Zhihua Wen,Xi Wang,Quntian Fang,Dongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出EAACD方法，利用MoE模型高层专家的激活模式差异进行对比解码，有效缓解幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉缓解方法（提示工程、模型优化）要么难以改变模型内部知识，要么跨领域泛化差；对比解码虽有效但仅探索了Transformer架构，未研究MoE模型。

Method: 1. 实证发现MoE共享专家层不存在层间差异，但高层专家在事实与非事实输出上激活模式不同；2. 将高层专家按置信度和一致性分为高可靠组和低可靠组；3. 通过注意力与掩码增强低可靠专家的幻觉输出作为负参考，与高可靠组进行对比解码。

Result: 在四个数据集上优于所有基线方法。

Conclusion: EAACD有效利用MoE模型的高层专家差异缓解幻觉，扩展了对比解码在非Transformer架构上的应用。

Abstract: Existing LLM hallucination mitigation methods, including prompt engineering and model optimization, either hardly alter models'internal knowledge or have poor cross-domain generalization. Contrastive decoding mitigates hallucinations by using layer-wise differences in LLMs. However, prior studies only explore transformer-based models (e.g., GPT), ignoring other effective frameworks like mixture-of-experts (MoE) models. Since MoE alters the traditional transformer architecture, we conduct empirical studies to investigate whether similar layer-wise differences exist in MoEs. Our results show that they do not exist in MoE with shared experts; nevertheless, across different MoEs, higher layers exhibit distinct expert activation patterns between factual and non-factual outputs. Building on these, we propose EAACD, an expert-aware adaptive contrast decoding that uses expert differences in MoE's higher layers to mitigate hallucinations on QA tasks. EAACD splits high-layer experts into a higher-reliability group and several lower-reliability groups based on their confidence and consistency. It contrasts the higher-reliability group's prediction with each lower-reliability group's prediction to calibrate the model's original predictions. To strengthen this contrast, EAACD amplifies hallucinations from lower-reliability experts via attention and masking to provide stronger negative references. EAACD outperforms all baselines on four datasets.

</details>


### [98] [Human-in-the-Loop Large Language Model Framework for Identification of Cutaneous Immune-Related Adverse Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20428)
*Charles Lu,Olivia Burke,Debby Cheng,Adam Kashlan,Caitlyn Duffy,Zeyun Lu,Lirit Fuksman,Jin Ning Tian,Andrew Sedlack,Priya Katyal,Eudora Lee,Ralina Karagenova,Chuck Lin,Kun-Hsing Yu,Nicole LeBoeuf,Alexander Gusev,Yevgeniy R. Semenov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种检索增强、多智能体、人在回路的大语言模型驱动框架，用于从临床记录中检测皮肤免疫相关不良事件。相比人工审查，该框架将F1分数从0.77提升至0.88，Cohen's kappa从0.50提升至0.82，并将平均审查时间缩短约一半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人工审查皮肤免疫相关不良事件（cirAEs）的临床记录耗时且不一致，需要一种更准确、可扩展且透明的方法来提取不良事件数据。

Method: 采用检索增强生成（RAG）与多智能体架构相结合的大语言模型框架，并引入人在回路（human-in-the-loop）机制，从临床笔记中自动化检测cirAEs。

Result: LLM辅助工作流相比纯人工审查，F1分数从0.77提升至0.88，Cohen's kappa从0.50提升至0.82，平均审查时间缩短约一半。

Conclusion: 该框架验证了LLM在识别跨器官系统免疫相关毒性方面的可行性，为实现准确、可扩展且透明的不良事件数据提取提供了范例。

Abstract: This study evaluated a retrieval-augmented, multi-agent large language model (LLM)-driven, human-in-the-loop framework for detecting cutaneous immune-related adverse events (cirAEs) from clinical notes. Compared with unassisted manual review, the LLM-assisted workflow improved accuracy (F1 = 0.88 vs 0.77), inter-rater agreement measured by Cohen's kappa (kappa = 0.82 vs 0.50), and reduced average review time by approximately half. This framework pilots how LLMs can be applied to identify immune-related toxicities across organ systems and, more broadly, enable accurate, scalable, and transparent adverse event data extraction.

</details>


### [99] [Is MoE Routing a Huffman Code? Discovering the Frequency-Diversity Law in Chain-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20427)
*Ching-Chieh Tsao,Zhuoyi Lin,Wenya Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现MoE路由本质上是哈夫曼编码的体现，提出频率-多样性定律，并设计子集差异剪枝策略消除冗余，主张MoE应转向最小描述长度最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示MoE路由背后的黑箱原理，发现其并非简单选择，而是信息论层面的哈夫曼编码机制。

Method: 提出频率-多样性定律解释路由行为；设计子集差异剪枝策略消除功能冗余专家。

Result: 剪枝不损害推理能力，反而释放模型潜在的哈夫曼效率，使路由路径更精简、高密度。

Conclusion: MoE应超越强制负载均衡，追求最小描述长度最优性，将高频信息分配短路由编码，低频信息分配长且多样的路由编码。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts architectures have revolutionized scaling, yet the underlying logic of their routing remains a black box. In this paper, we uncover a fundamental governing principle: MoE routing is not merely selection, but a manifestation of Huffman Coding. We introduce the Frequency-Diversity Law, revealing that state-of-the-art models, such as Phi-3.5-MoE and Gemma-4-27B-A4B, spontaneously act as information-theoretic engines. These models allocate sparse expert resources for common tokens while invoking high-diversity expert committees for rare, complex tasks found in chain-of-thought trajectories. However, we identify a critical redundancy trap in Qwen3.5-35B-A3B: when effective sparsity (k/E_eff) is sufficiently low, load-balancing inadvertently imposes functional redundancy, masking the underlying Huffman efficiency signal. To bridge this gap, we propose Subset Difference Pruning, a surgical strategy to eliminate functional duplicates. We demonstrate that pruning does not degrade reasoning; instead, it unleashes the model's latent Huffman efficiency, forcing the logic to collapse into streamlined, high-density paths. Our findings suggest that the next generation of MoEs should move beyond forced load-balancing toward Minimum Description Length (MDL) optimality, assigning shorter expert-routing codes to high-frequency information and longer, more diverse codes to low-frequency information, thereby transforming routing from a heuristic into a principled compression engine.

</details>


### [100] [More Is Not More: What Matters for Diversity in LLM Opinions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20429)
*Qiyang Yao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过析因实验系统研究了影响LLM输出多样性的两个关键维度（输入条件与交互架构），发现：人物角色深度并非单调提升多样性、不同交互架构探索互补的语义空间、结构化干预远优于低成本替代方案（如提高采样温度）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM用于模拟人类意见时存在系统性意见同质化问题，虽然已有多种干预手段但评估碎片化、无法分离不同组件的贡献，亟需系统性的科学理解。

Method: 设计析因实验，分离两个主要干预维度：输入条件（通过人物角色深度操作化）和交互架构。在100个真实用户开放性问题、7个模型上评估，使用多种互补多样性指标。

Result: 1) 人物角色细节并非单调提升多样性，初始角色设定已带来大部分增益，添加人口统计细节反而可能降低某些模型的多样性；2) 不同交互架构探索大量不重叠的意见区域，组合多个架构优于优化单一架构；3) 低成本替代方案（提高温度、添加多样性指令）效果远弱于结构化干预。

Conclusion: 多样性并非沿着单一维度扩展的产物，而是对干预的结构形式和组合高度敏感。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to simulate diverse human opinions in open-ended tasks such as synthetic surveys, focus group modeling, and public opinion prediction. However, LLM outputs exhibit systematic opinion homogenization. Practitioners have explored various interventions to increase diversity, but the landscape remains fragmented: different methods are evaluated in isolation with incomparable metrics, and in practice they are typically deployed and upgraded simultaneously, making it difficult to attribute gains to specific components. To advance a more scientific understanding of LLM output diversity, we design a factorial experiment that separates two primary intervention dimensions: input conditioning (operationalized through persona depth) and interaction architecture. We evaluate all conditions on 100 real-user open-ended questions across 7 models, measuring diversity with multiple complementary metrics. Our findings challenge several common assumptions. First, more persona detail does not monotonically increase diversity. The initial step of persona conditioning already captures the majority of the gain, while further elaboration with demographic detail does not consistently improve and can reduce diversity on some models. Second, rather than seeking a single best interaction architecture, we find that different architectures explore largely non-overlapping opinion regions. Combining multiple architectures yields broader coverage than optimizing any one. Third, commonly attempted low-cost alternatives such as raising sampling temperature and adding diversity instructions produce negligible effects compared to structured interventions. Overall, our work demonstrates that diversity is not a product of scaling along any single dimension, but is highly sensitive to the structural form and combination of interventions.

</details>


### [101] [LLM-INSTRUCT at UZH Shared Task 2026: Constraint-Aware Retrieval and Selective Debate for Paragraph-Level Argument Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20430)
*Phuong Huu Vu Tran,Long Minh Vo,Son Nguyen Minh Le,Hoang Van*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了LLM-INSTRUCT系统，用于联合国和教科文组织决议的段落级论点挖掘任务，通过约束结构化预测方法（包括元数据感知检索、约束解码、多智能体辩论和模式验证），在ArgMining 2026的UZH共享任务中获得了整体第一名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对联合国和教科文组织决议中段落级论点挖掘这一复杂结构化预测任务，需要在严格的JSON模式约束下，使用不超过8B参数的开源模型完成段落类型分类、141个官方标签的子集预测和有向关系预测。

Method: 1) 元数据感知的稠密检索缩小候选标签空间；2) 带维度上限的约束解码；3) 对不确定案例引入三智能体辩论分支；4) 最终输出模式验证。

Result: 官方排行榜总体排名第1，F1得分第1，LLM-as-a-Judge第5。开发过程中Task 1b Micro-F1从35.83%提升至40.08%，Task 2得分维持在4.421。

Conclusion: 核心经验是：在生成之前缩小决策空间既能提高准确率，也能增强提交的鲁棒性。

Abstract: We present LLM-INSTRUCT, the winning system for the UZH Shared Task at ArgMining 2026 on paragraph-level argument mining in UN and UNESCO resolutions. The task requires paragraph-type classification, prediction of a subset of 141 official tags, and directed relation prediction under a strict JSON schema setting using only open-weight models up to 8B parameters. We frame the task as constrained structured prediction. The system first narrows the candidate tag space with metadata-aware dense retrieval, then applies constrained decoding with per-dimension caps, escalates only uncertain cases to a three-agent debate branch, and finally validates the output schema. On the official leaderboard, LLM-INSTRUCT ranked 1st overall, with 1st in F1 and 5th in LLM-as-a-Judge. During development, our configuration search further improved Task 1b Micro-F1 from 35.83% to 40.08% while keeping the internal Task 2 score at 4.421. The main lesson is simple: reducing the decision space before generation improves both accuracy and submission robustness. Our code and supporting scripts are publicly available at: https://github.com/LLM-Instruct-at-UZH-Shared-Task-2026/Method

</details>


### [102] [Skill-Contracted Agents for Evidence-Aware Materials Literature Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20431)
*Bixuan Li,Yu Liu,Shuo Shi,Xiaoya Huang,Peng Kang,Lei Zheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AlphaAgent是一个技能驱动的智能体框架，通过将检索式问答与论文级报告生成解耦（显式技能契约），显著提升了材料科学文献分析中复杂推理任务的表现。在40个材料科学问题的盲评中，AlphaAgent大幅超越基线系统，尤其在机理解释和可信度边界感知方面表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的检索增强生成（RAG）流水线难以在单一"检索-生成"架构中协调成分、工艺、表征和性能关系等多异构任务。现有方法无法有效解耦不同粒度的分析需求，导致复杂材料科学问题的分析质量受限。

Method: 提出AlphaAgent框架，核心是显式技能契约：1）检索技能：将用户请求重写为材料特定的搜索意图，查询包含30万+篇论文的索引（来自JCR冶金与冶金工程类别），证据不足时自动重构查询；2）报告生成技能：解析完整PDF生成结构化单篇分析报告和跨论文摘要。两个技能通过清晰的契约进行任务分离与协作。

Result: 在40个材料科学问题的盲评（一半需要深度分析推理）中，AlphaAgent在底层模型、文档索引和检索规模匹配的条件下，大幅优于基线系统，最大提升体现在机理解释和可信度边界感知两方面。

Conclusion: 显式的任务分离、精细化的检索意图以及基于证据感知的生成策略，能够有效提升基于大语言模型的材料科学文献分析能力。

Abstract: Materials science literature analysis requires simultaneous attention to composition, processing, characterization, and property relationships, yet conventional retrieval-augmented generation pipelines struggle to reconcile heterogeneous tasks within a single retrieve-then-generate architecture. Here we present AlphaAgent, a skill-driven agent framework that decouples retrieval-based question answering from paper-level report generation through explicit skill contracts. A dedicated retrieval skill rewrites user requests into material-specific search intents, queries a curated index of more than 300,000 papers from the Journal Citation Reports Metallurgy and Metallurgical Engineering category, and reformulates queries when initial evidence is insufficient. A separate report-generation skill parses full-text PDFs to produce structured per-paper analytical reports and cross-paper summaries. In a blind evaluation on 40 materials-science questions, half of which required deep analytical reasoning, AlphaAgent substantially outperformed a baseline system matched for underlying model, document index, and retrieval scale, with the largest gains in mechanistic explanation and awareness of credibility boundaries. These results indicate that explicit task separation, refined retrieval intent, and evidence-aware generation improve large-language-model-based literature analysis for materials research.

</details>


### [103] [Position: Natural Language Should Not Fully Replace Formal Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20432)
*Eitan Wagner,Elisha Rosensweig,Omri Abend*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文论证自然语言在低特异性任务中表现优异，而形式语言在高特异性任务中更有优势，两者应互补而非替代，提出了"特异性交叉定理"来定量分析这一权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对"自然语言将完全取代形式语言（如编程语言）"这一近期流行观点，作者认为这种说法忽视了自然语言固有的语义不明确性，需要通过形式化分析来澄清两种语言的适用边界。

Method: 提出了基于信息论的"任务特异性"定义，量化了用户需求对输出空间不确定性的缩减程度；并证明了"特异性交叉定理"，指出存在一个阈值，超过该阈值后直接用形式语言表达比用自然语言更高效。

Result: 通过图像生成、代码合成、音频制作等多模态案例分析，验证了自然语言在低特异性任务中成本更低，而形式语言在高特异性任务中更具优势。

Conclusion: 自然语言和形式语言是互补的工具，不应相互替代；未来应发展混合系统，让用户能够根据任务特异性在两种语言之间灵活切换。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models and their widespread adoption have prompted claims that natural language could entirely replace formal languages, such as programming languages for software design. In this position paper, we argue that this perspective overlooks fundamental linguistic properties of natural language, specifically that it is optimized for underspecification in open-ended contexts. We introduce a formal framework centered on *task specificity*, defining it as the information-theoretic reduction of uncertainty in an output space -- such as all possible images -- given a user's specific requirements. We prove a *specificity crossover theorem*, showing the existence of a threshold beyond which the cost to express formal requirements into natural language exceeds the cost of direct formal specification. By analyzing case studies across modalities, such as image generation, code synthesis, and audio production, we demonstrate that natural language excels at low specificity tasks, while formal languages are advantageous on tasks with stricter requirements. We conclude that natural and formal languages are complementary tools and advocate the development of hybrid systems that allow users to move across the specificity spectrum.

</details>


### [104] [Moir: Let the Model Direct Its Own Story for Robust Cross-Domain Knowledge Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20433)
*Jea Kwon,Jiwon Kim,Dong-kyum Kim,Meeyoung Cha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Moir方法，通过从模型自身解码分布中采样来估计保留协方差，解决知识编辑中数学和编程能力退化问题，在多个模型上显著提升编辑后的推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识编辑会导致模型核心能力（数学推理、编程）退化，而百科式回忆能力保持完好。作者发现这种不对称退化源于分布不匹配——现有协方差编辑器只保留参考语料子空间，无法捕捉SFT/DPO等后训练形成的操作分布。

Method: 从模型自身解码分布中采样估计保留协方差C。用单个随机词汇token启动生成，绕过指令遵循模板，暴露模型内化的更广泛子空间。无需外部数据，可作为任何协方差编辑器的即插即用组件。

Result: 在OLMo-2、Llama-3.1、Qwen-3（7-8B）上，结合MEMIT和AlphaEdit，在batch和sequential模式下均显著提升脆弱域的保留性能。Qwen3-8B经过20000次AlphaEdit批量编辑后，GSM8K准确率79.9%（Wikipedia基线仅10.9%）。

Conclusion: 将保留分布与模型的操作分布对齐是非破坏性知识编辑的关键因素，模型本身可能是已部署系统最易获取的分布来源。

Abstract: While language models remain frozen at their training state, the world evolves continuously. Knowledge editing has emerged as a key alternative to full retraining, but its deployment is bottlenecked by the erosion of core capabilities: mathematical and programmatic reasoning collapse while encyclopedic recall remains intact. We trace this asymmetric degradation to a distributional mismatch. Covariance-based editors preserve only the subspaces spanned by their reference corpus, but fail to capture the operative distribution shaped by post-training such as SFT and DPO. Static external corpora, including Wikipedia and even the original pretraining mixture, cannot recover this shifted manifold. We propose Moir, which estimates the preservation covariance $C$ directly from the model itself by sampling from its own decoding distribution. Seeding generation with a single random vocabulary token bypasses the instruction-following templates that otherwise dominate sampled outputs, exposing the broader subspaces the model has internalized. Moir requires no external data and serves as a drop-in component for any covariance-based editor, a practical advantage given that the pre- and post-training corpora of most modern LLMs are not publicly accessible. Across OLMo-2, Llama-3.1, and Qwen-3 (7-8B), under both MEMIT and AlphaEdit and in batch and sequential regimes, Moir consistently extends preservation in the most vulnerable domains, most strikingly on Qwen3-8B after 20,000 AlphaEdit batch edits, it retains 79.9% GSM8K accuracy compared to 10.9% with the Wikipedia baseline. These results suggest that aligning the preservation distribution with the model's operative distribution is a key factor in non-destructive editing, and that the model itself may be the most accessible source of that distribution for deployed systems.

</details>


### [105] [Break Through the Compression Bottleneck: From Theory to Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20434)
*Xiusheng Huang,Lu Wang,Yequan Wang,Jun Zhao,Kang Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次从数学上证明低秩分解与量化两种模型压缩方法并非正交，二者组合会导致显著性能下降，并提出对角粘附方法(DAM)来有效结合二者并减轻性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型参数量持续增长，需要有效的模型压缩方法来降低计算和内存开销。现有压缩方法存在瓶颈：压缩比增大时性能显著下降。研究者认为低秩分解与量化是正交的，即组合不会引入额外误差，但这种假设缺乏理论验证。

Method: 1) 首次从数学上证明低秩分解与量化是非正交的；2) 通过一系列大语言模型实验验证理论发现；3) 提出对角粘附方法(DAM)，有效结合两种方法并减轻性能损失。

Result: 实验证实低秩分解与量化确实是非正交的，二者组合会导致显著的性能退化。提出的DAM方法能够有效缓解这种性能损失。

Conclusion: 该研究为模型压缩提供了深刻的理论见解，为未来相关研究奠定了坚实的理论和实验基础，挑战了"两种方法正交"的普遍假设。

Abstract: As the parameter size of language models continues to grow, effective model compression is required to reduce their computational and memory overhead. Existing compression methods suffer from bottleneck issues: when the compression ratio is increased, performance degrades significantly. Low-rank decomposition and quantization are two prominent compression methods that have been proven to significantly reduce the computational and memory requirements of Large Language Models (LLMs) while maintaining model accuracy. Evidently, combining these two methods will break through the existing compression bottleneck. However, how these two methods interact when combined remains a critical question for developers, as many assume they are orthogonal, meaning their combination would not introduce additional errors beyond those independently introduced by each method. This paper provides the first mathematical proof that low-rank decomposition and quantization are non-orthogonal. We validate these findings through a series of experiments on large language models. Our results demonstrate that these methods are non-orthogonal, and their combination leads to significant performance degradation. Importantly, we propose a novel approach Diagonal Adhesive Method (DAM), which can effectively combine the two methods and mitigate the performance loss. Our research provides deep insights into model compression and lays a solid theoretical and experimental foundation for future related studies.

</details>


### [106] [Making Open-Source Text LLM Watermarks Durable Against Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20435)
*Luisa Scharff,Thibaud Gloaguen,Robin Staab,Martin Vechev*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种对抗训练方法，使得开源大模型（OSM）的文本水印在模型合并后仍能保持鲁棒性，在TPR@1%FPR上最高提升51个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源大模型虽性能接近闭源模型，但容易在后续微调（尤其是模型合并）中被移除水印，需要设计能抵御模型合并的水印方案。

Method: 提出Merge-Adversarial Training，一种对抗训练算法，将文本水印蒸馏到模型权重中，同时使其对后续模型合并具有鲁棒性。

Result: 在SLERP合并下TPR@1%FPR最高提升51个百分点，平均提升25个百分点，且不损害模型下游能力。在三种主流合并算法和多种实际合并场景下均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 对抗训练是提升开源大模型水印在模型合并等后训练修改中持久性的可靠方法。

Abstract: Open-source LLMs (OSMs)arereaching near state-of-the-art performance, prompting prior works to trace the text they generate by embedding text watermarking algorithms directly into their weights. Yet, OSMs are subject to post-training modifications, which has been shown to remove the watermark. Model merging in particular, a prominent method used for combining expert knowledge and preventing catastrophic forgetting, strongly removes such OSM watermarks. A key question is how to enable OSM watermarks that survive subsequent merging. In this work, we show for the first time how to design an OSM watermark that is durable against model merging. We propose Merge-Adversarial Training, an adversarial training algorithm to distill text watermarks into model weights while being robust to subsequent model merging. Our approach consistently outperforms all baselines (e.g. with SLERP up to +51 percentage points (pp) TPR@1%FPR with +25 pp on average) while preserving downstream capabilities. We also for the first time evaluate OSM watermarks against realistic merge scenarios, representing common use-cases such as combining expert capabilities or preventing catastrophic forgetting, and with 3 prominent merging algorithms. More broadly, our findings suggest that adversarial training is a reliable approach for increasing OSM watermark durability against post-training modifications.

</details>


### [107] [Routing Subspaces: Auditing Evaluation-to-Deployment Mismatch in Fine-Tuned Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20436)
*Phongsakon Mark Konrad,Toygar Tanyel,Serkan Ayvaz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现，安全评估中假设测试行为反映实际使用行为，但微调会打破这一假设。作者通过路径修补找到编码评估与实际使用差异的稳定内部位置，并通过对该位置进行干预来缩小评估-部署差距，在12个模型-行为设置中的10个有效（其中120个以上配对问题的8个设置中有6个成功）。但单个坐标审计在谄媚行为等场景下不足，且该方法只是诊断工具而非训练防御或安全保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全评估通常假设测试时的行为反映实际使用时的行为，但微调会破坏这一假设。检测点可能在评估风格提示下看起来已修复，但在普通使用提示下仍存在问题。现有输出评分方法能发现这种不匹配但无法定位问题所在，因此需要探究这种差异是否编码在模型内部的稳定位置。

Method: 作者通过路径修补在中间深度窗口定位到编码评估-部署差异的内部位置，然后对该位置的激活进行配对对比，并在保留提示上修改该坐标。实验在五个全矩阵指令微调模型上进行，检验了12个模型-行为设置。

Result: 该干预方法在12个模型-行为设置中的10个成功缩小了评估到部署的差距；在120个及以上配对问题的8个设置中，6个有效。两个无效的设置（均为谄媚行为）表明单坐标审计在差异是高秩或被深度启发式遗漏时不够充分。第五个模型支持定位和编辑溯源检查，部署框架下的变化率最多为6.1个百分点。

Conclusion: 该方法可作为微调检查点的诊断工具，但不是训练时的防御手段，也不能保证部署安全。当内部差异编码高于单一维度或超出特定深度时，需要更复杂的审计方法。

Abstract: Safety evaluations often assume that behavior observed during testing reflects behavior in ordinary use, but fine-tuning can break this assumption. A checkpoint can appear fixed under evaluation-style prompts while the same behavior persists under ordinary-use prompts. Output scores reveal this mismatch but do not locate it. We investigate whether the distinction is encoded in a stable internal site and introduce an approach that fits a paired activation contrast at a path-patching-informed mid-depth window, then modifies the resulting coordinate on held-out prompts. The intervention closes the evaluation-to-deployment gap in ten of twelve model--behavior settings (six of the eight settings with $n{\geq}120$ paired questions) across four full-matrix instruction-tuned model instances; a fifth model supports localization and edit-provenance checks, and deployment-framed rates change by at most $6.1$pp. The two flat cells, both sycophancy, indicate that a single-coordinate audit is not sufficient when the installed distinction is higher-rank or missed by the depth heuristic. The audit is a diagnostic for fine-tuned checkpoints, not a training-time defense or a guarantee of deployment safety.

</details>


### [108] [TopoGuard: Graph Theory Based Defenses Against Split-Knowledge Attacks on RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20437)
*Chahana Dahal,Zuobin Xiong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了检索增强生成（RAG）系统中的分知识攻击，并提出了一种基于图论的新型防御框架TopoGuard。该攻击利用多个看似无害的文档组合误导大模型，超出了传统逐文档过滤器的检测能力。TopoGuard通过构建文档语义相似图并检测恶意拓扑结构，有效识别此类攻击。实验表明，TopoGuard-λ₂+Entity在HotpotQA数据集上以1%假阳性率检测出32.6%的攻击，是LlamaGuard-2-8B的21倍，且推理延迟低于毫秒级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统依赖多文档聚合，但检索到的文档带来了新的安全威胁——攻击者可以注入多个看似无害、组合后却能误导模型的恶意文档（分知识攻击）。此类攻击结构上对逐文档过滤器不可见，现有检测手段无效，因此亟需一种能捕捉文档间关联关系的检测方法。

Method: 提出TopoGuard家族方法：基于图论，从检索文档构建语义相似图，通过检测图中的恶意拓扑结构（如异常连通性）来识别分知识攻击。包括多种变体（如TopoGuard-λ₂+Entity），利用图拉普拉斯特征值等理论工具进行鲁棒检测，并在理论分析基础上保证对噪声输入的有效性。

Result: 在HotpotQA等数据集上，TopoGuard-λ₂+Entity在1%假阳性率下召回率达32.6%，显著优于LlamaGuard-2-8B的1.5%（21倍提升）；推理延迟低于毫秒，远快于基于大模型的检测系统；在自适应攻击和跨域良性查询下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: TopoGuard通过图拓扑分析有效填补了RAG系统中针对多文档协同攻击的检测空白，具有高效、鲁棒的优势，适合部署到生产环境中。后续工作可探索更复杂的图结构检测及更多攻击变体。

Abstract: Production Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) systems rely on aggregating multiple external documents to answer complex queries. However, the retrieved documents introduce a new threat surface that can be exploited to launch split-knowledge attacks. In this attack, the adversary injects documents that are individually benign but create false associations when combined and fed to language models. This paper shows that the new attack is structurally invisible to existing per-document filters, like LlamaGuard. To address this issue in RAG, this work introduces TopoGuard, a family of graph theory-based methods specifically targeting the split-knowledge attacks by building a semantic similarity graph from retrieved documents and detecting contexts with malicious topology. Grounded on the theoretical analysis, the TopoGuard family has been proven to be effective and robust even with noisy inputs. Extensive experiments are conducted on two retrieval datasets and compared with multiple baseline methods. Specifically, the TopoGuard-$λ_2$+Entity catches 21$\times$ more attacks than LlamaGuard-2-8B at 1\% FPR (32.6\% vs 1.5\% recall) on the HotpotQA dataset. Compared with production RAG detection systems using large language models, the proposed TopoGuard variants run efficiently at sub-millisecond latency and stay robust under adaptive adversaries and benign cross-domain queries.

</details>


### [109] [Preference Tuning as Spectral Update Reorganization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20438)
*Peiyan Zhang,Haibo Jin,Liying Kang,Haohan Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过谱分解方法揭示了偏好对齐训练（RLHF/DPO等）中参数更新的内在结构：更新量会自发地分解为“头部”（主导行为偏移）和“尾部”（残差信息），头部主导了模型行为变化但单独学习不够充分，尾部单独学习效果微弱但不可或缺，表明偏好后训练本质上是参数更新的结构化重组而非单一行为修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有偏好对齐研究主要关注模型最终行为变化，但对参数更新过程本身的结构和组成知之甚少，论文希望打开这一"黑箱"，理解偏好优化在参数空间中的内在组织方式。

Method: 对LoRA形式的偏好优化更新量进行谱分解，将更新量的谱成分提取为可插拔模块，通过隔离、重组和直接干预的方式分析头部与尾部的功能差异。

Result: 1）偏好更新量呈现"头部-尾部"的谱组织，头部紧凑且主导端点行为偏移，尾部为异质残差；2）头部负责可见的行为偏离（如对齐），尾部单独作用微弱；3）跨实验混合适配器时，行为由头部来源决定（体现运行级求解器偏差）；4）头部单独学习有意义但不充分（尤其在OOD行为上），无尾部则无法恢复完整解。

Conclusion: 偏好后训练应被理解为参数更新的结构化重组过程，而非单一的行为修正；对齐增益与覆盖损失与更新量自身的谱组织方式密切相关。

Abstract: Preference-based post-training is usually understood through endpoint behavior, yet the learned update that produces this behavior remains largely opaque. We study RLHF and related preference optimization through the spectral structure of their induced parameter updates. By decomposing effective LoRA updates and reloading their spectral components as plug-in modules, we turn preference-induced updates into objects that can be isolated, recomposed, and directly intervened on. Across model families, optimization algorithms, and supervision regimes, these updates consistently develop a spectral head--tail organization. A compact head emerges early and carries the dominant endpoint shift, while a heterogeneous residual tail remains. The split is functional rather than merely descriptive. Plug-in intervention shows that the head accounts for the visible behavioral departure from the base model, while the tail is weak in isolation. Cross-run recomposition further shows that mixed adapters follow the source of the head, indicating that the head carries run-level solver bias. This endpoint dominance does not imply learning sufficiency. Head-only learning is non-vacuous but fails to recover the full solution, especially on out-of-distribution behavior. Tail-only learning yields little visible gain, yet the full solution is not recovered without the tail. These findings recast preference post-training as structured update reorganization rather than a monolithic behavioral correction, and suggest that alignment gain and coverage loss are tied to how the learned update itself is organized.

</details>


### [110] [AsymVerify at SemEval-2026 Task 6: Asymmetric Confidence-Gated Verification for Political Evasion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20439)
*Sebastien Kawada*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了AsymVerify，一个置信门控验证系统，用于政治逃避回答的三分类任务，在SemEval-2026 Task 6上以0.85 Macro F1排名第二。系统对低置信度预测选择性应用升级或降级验证，以较少额外推理成本提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 政治逃避回答难以检测，因为逃避性答案往往表面合作但缺乏具体承诺，需要更精细的自动分类方法。

Method: 采用两阶段设计：先对问答对进行单次三分类，再对低置信度预测选择性应用非对称验证——降级验证（明确回复/明确不回复→模糊）和升级验证（模糊→明确分类）。置信度门控控制额外推理成本。

Result: 在D_eval上Macro F1=0.85，41支队伍中排名第二；在D_dev上相比单次分类提升+17.1 Macro F1，平均1.48次调用/样本；升级验证器在所有测试的LLM后端上提升+6.8至+15.2 Macro F1。

Conclusion: 非对称双验证器设计有效处理了以模糊边界为中心的分类错误，置信度门控在提升性能的同时保持了较低的计算开销。
