CultureTalk-ID introduces three complementary tasks, namely dialogue-based multiple-choice cultural commonsense reasoning, culturally faithful machine translation, and language steering, which jointly probe whether LLMs can understand, transfer, and generate culturally grounded language.

</details>


### [147] [Sample-Efficient Learning from Agent Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21051)
*Chenhui Gou,Haoqin Tu,Yunhao Fang,Jianfei Cai,Hamid Rezatofighi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Experience Distillation方法，通过将智能体的交互历史蒸馏到模型权重中，在不额外消耗环境样本的情况下，保留至少64.8%的上下文学习收益，相比直接微调（仅保留3.8%）和强化学习基线（样本效率提升9.6倍）表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实场景中智能体学习常受限于高成本的环境交互（如耗时实验或人工反馈）。上下文学习虽样本高效，但经验一旦移出上下文效果即消失。本文旨在将交互历史内化到模型权重中，同时不牺牲环境样本效率。

Method: 提出Experience Distillation方法，利用已收集的交互经验，将上下文学习中的经验蒸馏到模型参数中，无需额外环境交互。

Result: 在749个软件工程任务和6个文本冒险游戏上，Experience Distillation保留了至少64.8%的上下文学习收益，而直接监督微调仅保留3.8%。相比经典强化学习基线，在至少9.6倍更少的环境样本下达到匹配性能。

Conclusion: Experience Distillation能有效将上下文学习经验内化为模型权重，显著提升样本效率，为低成本智能体学习提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Real-world agent learning is often constrained by costly environment interactions, such as running time-consuming experiments or obtaining human feedback. In-context learning offers a highly sample-efficient way for agents to learn from their own interaction histories, but its gains disappear once that experience is removed from the context. Separately, context distillation provides a mechanism for internalizing contextual information into model weights. However, applying it to agents' interaction histories without sacrificing environment sample efficiency remains underexplored. We term this problem Experience Distillation and develop an implementation that requires no further environment interaction beyond the collected experience. Experiments on 749 curated software-engineering tasks and six text-adventure games show that it retains at least 64.8\% of the gains from in-context learning across both domains, whereas direct supervised fine-tuning on the collected experience recovers only 3.8\%. Compared with classical reinforcement-learning baselines, in-context learning from trial-and-error experience followed by Experience Distillation matches their performance with at least \(9.6\times\) fewer environment samples.

</details>


### [148] [QuantiBias: Benchmarking Quantization-Induced Bias in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21063)
*Emilio Ferrara*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 量化模型在标准安全评估中表现良好，但在开放性问题中会暴露出显著的刻板印象偏差（约24-27%），QuantiBias基准揭示了这一漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化被认为无害且很少重新检查安全性，但研究发现其主要副作用是增加了标准安全评估无法检测出的偏差。

Method: 提出QuantiBias基准，包含生成式多语言刻板印象探测、拒绝和多项选择控制、推理对比以及内容严重性评级，并在Qwen和Gemma等多个模型上测试。

Result: 量化模型通过所有标准安全检查，但在开放性问题中约24-27%的答案表现出刻板印象偏差；推理可减轻部分模型家族的偏差但对其他无效。

Conclusion: 量化模型必须重新评估开放性偏差，不能仅依赖已通过的短格式安全检测。

Abstract: Almost every large language model that reaches a broad audience is quantized: trained in full precision, then compressed for efficiency. This step is assumed harmless and its safety is rarely re-checked. We find its principal side effect is increased bias that standard safety evaluation misses. Holding the model, its training, and the prompts fixed, a quantized model still refuses harmful requests, still avoids over-refusing benign prompts, and still selects the unbiased multiple-choice answer. Yet asked an open-ended question, the same model volunteers stereotypes in all eight languages we probe, in roughly one in four open-ended answers under an independent judge (~24% to ~27% across the compression ladder): it passes every standard check and still reaches users measurably more biased. The selective gap is a robust finding; whether open-ended bias further increases with compression is less certain, sensitive to the judge that scores it. We address both with \textbf{QuantiBias}, a benchmark that pairs a generative, multilingual stereotype probe with the refusal and multiple-choice controls that isolate open-ended generation, contrasts each build with and without reasoning, and rates the content severity of what it generates. Across two backbone models (Qwen and Gemma), a five-family screen, and eight benchmarks, quantizers allocate their extra precision by capability data that carries no bias-prevention signal, and reasoning before answering roughly halves the effect on some families while doing nothing on others. A quantized build must be re-evaluated for open-ended bias, not only on the short-form safeguards it already passes.

</details>


### [149] [PrefReward: Learning User Preference Matrix for Personalized Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21067)
*Yue Wu,Chengbing Wang,Yimeng Bai,Xiaoyan Zhao,Yang Zhang,Fuli Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出PrefReward框架，通过结构化偏好矩阵显式建模用户风格，并作为奖励信号指导LLM生成个性化内容，在LongLaMP数据集上优于非个性化和检索基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM个性化方法依赖模型参数的隐式表示，难以解释用户偏好且无法有效处理长上下文依赖。

Method: 两阶段框架：1) 提取用户特定偏好矩阵（结构化总结风格倾向）；2) 基于KL散度的奖励函数在解码阶段引导生成。

Result: 在LongLaMP数据集上，PrefReward在生成质量和个性化可解释性方面均优于非个性化和检索基线。

Conclusion: 结构化显式偏好建模可有效提升个性化生成的可解释性与长上下文处理能力。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable ability in generating personalized content by leveraging user histories and contextual cues. However, most existing personalization approaches rely on implicit representations within model parameters, making it difficult to interpret user-specific preferences or effectively handle long-context dependencies. To address these challenges, we propose PrefReward, a novel preference-aware generative framework that explicitly models user styles through a structured preference matrix and integrates it into the decoding process as a reward signal. PrefReward consists of two stages: (1) extracting a user-specific preference matrix that summarizes individual stylistic tendencies, and (2) using the matrix to guide generation via a KL-divergence-based reward function. Experiments on the LongLaMP dataset show that PrefReward outperforms non-personalized and retrieval-based baselines in both generation quality and personalization interpretability.

</details>


### [150] [One More Turn, Less Regret: A Regret-Based Multi-Turn Benchmark for LLMs' Clarification Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21143)
*Minh Ngoc Ta,My Anh Tran Nguyen,Duong D. Nguyen,Yuxia Wang,Preslav Nakov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RegretBench，一个基于遗憾的多轮对话澄清基准，从策略行为角度评估LLM助手在模糊用户请求下的澄清能力，而非仅评估单个问题的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方式只关注澄清问题的质量，忽略了澄清作为序列决策过程的策略性（何时问、问什么、何时停），无法全面衡量模型在模糊场景下的表现。

Method: 提出RegretBench基准，包含隐藏意图的模糊设定、基于语义状态追踪的自由交互，以及引入遗憾目标来衡量模型相对于参考澄清策略的价值损失。

Result: 在开放域QA和产品推荐场景中，不同模型在最终成功率相近的情况下，在效率、对用户行为的鲁棒性和停止决策上存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 有效的澄清不仅需要提出合理的问题，更需要在正确时机提问，并在用户意图明确后及时停止。

Abstract: Ambiguous user requests make clarification a sequential decision problem for conversational LLM assistants: they must decide whether to ask, what to ask, when to stop, and when to answer. We introduce RegretBench, a multi-turn benchmark that evaluates clarification as policy behavior rather than isolated question quality. RegretBench provides a hidden-intent formulation of ambiguity, supports free-form interaction grounded in semantic-state tracking, and introduces a regret-based objective that measures how much value a model loses relative to a reference clarification policy. Experiments on open-domain QA and product recommendation scenarios show that final success alone is insufficient, as models with similar accuracy can differ substantially in efficiency, robustness to user behaviors, and stopping decisions. By jointly measuring intent resolution, interaction cost, ineffective clarification, and regret, RegretBench reveals whether models clarify usefully and efficiently. Our results show that effective clarification requires more than plausible questions: models must ask the right question at the right time and stop once the user's intended meaning is clear.

</details>


### [151] [Progressive Cramming: Reliable Token Compression and What It Reveals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21231)
*Dmitrii Tarasov,Timofei Lashukov,Elizaveta Goncharova,Andrey Kuznetsov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出渐进式压缩方法，通过逐token扩展前缀至无法完美重建为止，发现压缩嵌入仅占据低维结构，虽能完美重建但严重损害模型性能，证明完美重建≠有意义压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Token压缩方法在固定预算和99%准确率阈值下，无法区分残差误差是优化失败还是理论极限，需要更精细的方法来探索压缩边界。

Method: 提出渐进式压缩（progressive cramming）：逐token增长目标前缀，在固定优化预算下停止于无法重建的临界点；并通过因果注意力击穿干预分析嵌入与模型的交互机制。

Result: 1）渐进轨迹占据嵌入空间的低维结构；2）前置压缩嵌入导致多项选择基准准确率持续下降，生成评估下能力几乎完全崩溃；3）注意力击穿实验表明退化源于嵌入与模型早期层的交互。

Conclusion: 完美重建可通过脆弱的导向（而非可迁移语义）实现，但不足以构成有意义的压缩；渐进式压缩可作为研究压缩极限的有效工具。

Abstract: Token cramming compresses sequences into learned embeddings with near-perfect reconstruction, but fixed token budgets and 99\% accuracy thresholds leave it unclear whether residual errors reflect optimization failures or fundamental limits. We introduce progressive cramming, which grows the target prefix token-by-token, stopping only when reconstruction is no longer achievable within a fixed optimization budget. Progressive trajectories occupy low-dimensional structure in embedding space. Prepending a crammed embedding causes a moderate but consistent accuracy drop on multiple-choice benchmarks even with the original prefix in context, and collapses capability almost entirely under generative evaluation. Causal attention-knockout interventions trace this degradation to the embedding's interactions in the model's early layers. These results position progressive cramming as a tool for studying compression limits and show that perfect reconstruction - achievable through brittle steering rather than transferable semantics - is insufficient for meaningful compression.

</details>


### [152] [slang.gr as a Large-Scale Crowdsourced Resource for Non-Standard Greek](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21255)
*Panagiotis Papadakos,Katerina Papantoniou,Dimitris Plexousakis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次对希腊非标准语言众包词典 slang.gr 进行大规模计算研究，通过构建多层分类体系分析希腊俚语的语言结构及贡献者社区行为，并提出基于社区的置信度评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 俚语是日常语言的核心组成部分，反映语言创造力、社会身份和文化变迁，但其动态性和非标准特性使得计算建模困难。目前缺乏对希腊俚语的大规模系统化计算研究。

Method: 将嘈杂的大众分类标签映射为结构化的多层分类体系（涵盖语义类别和社会语言学元数据）；在此基础上分析俚语语言结构和贡献者社区行为；提出融合用户角色、交互模式和审核信号的社区置信度评分。

Result: 希腊俚语高度集中于与人相关和评价性语言，表现出高形态创造力；社区参与极不均衡，用户活跃期短且存在重叠社区；基于分类体系的表示提高了置信度信号的可解释性。

Conclusion: 该工作将 slang.gr 确立为希腊非标准语言的计算资源，为社会语言学NLP、偏见分析及非正式语言在大语言模型中的研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Slang is a central component of everyday language, reflecting linguistic creativity, social identity, and cultural change, yet its dy- namic and non-standard nature makes it difficult to model computationally. We present the first large-scale computational study of slang.gr, a crowdsourced lexicon of Greek non-standard language, combining lexical content, user-generated tags, and interaction data. To enable the systematic analysis, we map noisy folksonomic tags to a structured multi-layer taxonomy capturing both semantic categories and sociolinguistic metadata. Using this representation, we analyze the linguistic structure of Greek slang and the behavior of its contributor community. We find that slang is strongly centered on person-related and evaluative language, exhibits high morphological creativity, and is shaped by highly skewed participation with short user lifespans and overlapping communities. Building on these signals, we introduce a community-based confidence score for definitions that integrates user roles, interaction patterns, and moderation signals. Our results show that taxonomy-based representations improve interpretability while retaining meaningful aspects of behavioral structure, enabling a more structured and interpretable analysis of confidence signals. Overall, this work establishes slang.gr as a computational resource for non-standard Greek and provides a foundation for sociolinguistic NLP, bias analysis, and the study of informal language in LLMs.

</details>


### [153] [A Comparative Evaluation of Embeddings and LLMs in a Greek Book Publisher Setting - The CUP Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21274)
*Katerina Papantoniou,Panagiotis Papadakos,Theodore Patkos,Dimitris Garefalakis,Nikos Vardakis,Dimitris Plexousakis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一个希腊语书籍检索基准CUP，包含868条目录记录和104条专家标注查询，实验发现混合检索方法表现最佳，多语言嵌入优于希腊语专用模型，不同查询类型适用不同检索方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺乏针对希腊语的书籍检索基准，现有评估多集中于英语，难以评估希腊语场景下不同检索方法（词汇、语义、噪声、跨语言）的实际表现。

Method: 构建了CUP基准数据集，对比了稀疏检索（BM25）、稠密检索（sentence-transformers）、混合检索和LLM辅助检索方法，并在命名实体、自然语言、噪声、跨语言和概念查询等维度进行细粒度分析。

Result: 混合检索整体最优；多语言嵌入优于希腊语专用模型；BM25在命名实体查询上表现优异，稠密和混合方法在自然语言、噪声、跨语言和概念查询上更好；字段感知提示和LLM摘要/后过滤有特定效果但成本高。

Conclusion: CUP基准为希腊语书籍检索提供了真实场景评估平台，揭示了不同检索方法在不同查询类型上的互补优势，为低资源语言检索研究提供了重要参考。

Abstract: We present CUP, a Greek book retrieval benchmark consisting of 868 catalog records and 104 expert-annotated queries with graded relevance judgments. We evaluate sparse (BM25), dense (sentence-transformers), hybrid, and LLM-assisted retrieval methods in this book-search setting. Multilingual embeddings outperform Greek-specific models, while hybrid retrieval performs best overall. A query-level analysis shows that BM25 excels at named-entity queries, while dense and hybrid methods improve natural-language, noisy, cross-lingual, and concept queries. Field-aware prompting has model-specific effects, while LLM TOC summarization improves TOC-only retrieval and LLM post-filtering improves early-stage retrieval at a high cost. Overall, CUP enables real-world evaluation of Greek retrieval across lexical, semantic, noisy, and cross-lingual queries.

</details>


### [154] [A Unified Moral-Value Dataset for Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21279)
*Zhaohui Zeng,Florian Mai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个可直接用于指令微调的道德价值观统一数据集，并探索了将其与通用任务数据集混合训练对模型性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指令微调数据集缺乏针对道德场景和行为的专门设计，如何使LLM与特定人类价值观对齐仍是一个开放问题。

Method: 将现有道德价值观数据集合并为统一语料库，并转换为指令-响应的格式；随后将通用任务数据集与道德数据集按不同比例混合进行训练。

Result: 混合训练在保持通用任务性能的同时，初步观察到混合比例会影响价值观导向任务的性能表现。

Conclusion: 该道德价值观数据集可作为指令微调的有用资源，为后续对齐研究提供支持。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have developed rapidly and become valuable tools in everyday life. However, how to align LLMs to a particular set of human values is still an open problem. Recent studies show that instruction tuning has strong potential for zero-shot tasks and may serve as an effective approach to addressing value alignment. Nevertheless, although many datasets for instruction tuning already exist, they are not specifically designed around moral scenarios and behaviors. We construct a unified moral-value dataset that can be directly used for instruction tuning. This dataset is built upon existing moral-value datasets by merging them into a unified corpus and converting them into an instruction-response format. We show that training on a mixed dataset combining general task datasets with our dataset preserves general-task performance, and we report preliminary observations on how the mixing ratio affects value-oriented task performance. Our work provides a moral-value dataset for instruction tuning and offers a useful resource for further alignment research. The dataset is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/teohzzh/value-for-instruction-tuning.

</details>


### [155] [news-crawler-LM: A Small Long-Context Model For High-Quality News Crawling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21284)
*Pascal Stolzenburg,Jonas Golde,Max Dallabetta,Alan Akbik*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 news-crawler-LM，一个基于 Fundus 库微调的小型长上下文语言模型，用于从新闻网页中提取结构化内容（标题、作者、日期、正文），在 HTML-to-Markdown 和 HTML-to-JSON 任务上优于强基线模型，但在纯文本提取上仅略优于规则方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新闻网页因 HTML 布局异构、标记不一致及大量模板（导航、广告）导致结构化提取困难；规则方法准确但需手动配置，大语言模型灵活但计算成本高，两者均存在局限。

Method: 使用 Fundus 新闻爬取库中人工验证的高质量提取数据，微调一个小型长上下文语言模型（news-crawler-LM），将原始 HTML 转换为纯文本或结构化 JSON 格式。

Result: HTML-to-Markdown 任务 BLEU +4.8、METEOR +6.1；HTML-to-JSON 任务 BLEU +2.2、METEOR +4.1；HTML-to-plaintext 任务在未见过的发布者上仅略优于规则方法。

Conclusion: news-crawler-LM 在结构化和半结构化提取任务上优势明显，但纯文本提取中相对于规则库的优势有限，模型和工件已开源。

Abstract: Extracting structured content from news pages remains challenging due to heterogeneous HTML layouts, inconsistent markup, and substantial boilerplate such as navigation elements and advertisements. Rule-based news crawlers can achieve high extraction accuracy by encoding site-specific structure, but require manual configuration in order to generalize to new publishers. Large language models provide a more flexible alternative by reducing the need for handcrafted rules, but their high computational cost limits practical deployment. In this paper, we introduce news-crawler-LM, a small long-context language model fine-tuned on high-quality, human-validated extractions from the Fundus news-crawling library. Our model converts raw HTML into plaintext and structured JSON, including fields such as headline, author, publication date, and article body. In our experiments, news-crawler-LM outperforms strong baselines in HTML-to-Markdown and HTML-to-JSON extraction, improving performance by +4.8 BLEU and +6.1 METEOR in the HTML-to-Markdown task, and by +2.2 BLEU and +4.1 METEOR in the HTML-to-JSON task. However, we also observe that our model only slightly better compared to other rule-based parsing libraries on the HTML-to-plaintext task in evaluations on previously unseen publishers. We release all models and artifacts to the research community.

</details>


### [156] [Adaptive Depth Sparse Framework: Similarity-Driven Resource Allocation for Pre-Trained LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21291)
*Yidu Wu,Xiang Wang,Kejie Zhao,Zhangchi Wang,Qinghai Guo,Xiaoying Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出AdaDSF框架，将预训练LLM转为深度稀疏模型，通过余弦相似度分析层贡献、轻量路由器选择重要token、特征保留对齐目标，在减少推理FLOPs的同时保持性能接近密集模型，优于MoD、D-LLM、DLO等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer架构推理成本高，现有加速方法依赖任务特定微调或从头训练，跨任务泛化受限，需要一种无需全量重训的通用稀疏化方法。

Method: 1) 基于层输入输出余弦相似度统计分配token保留比例；2) 轻量路由器选择每层重要token；3) 特征保留对齐目标使稀疏模型与密集模型的中间和最终表示匹配。

Result: 在GPT-NeoX和Qwen2.5的语言建模与常识推理任务上，显著降低推理FLOPs，性能接近密集模型，同等稀疏度下准确率下降小于MoD、D-LLM、DLO。

Conclusion: AdaDSF无需全量重训即可有效加速预训练LLM推理，兼顾效率与性能，具有良好跨任务泛化性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve strong generation and reasoning performance, but the Transformer architecture incurs high inference cost. Existing acceleration methods often rely on task-specific fine-tuning or training from scratch, increasing adaptation cost and limiting cross-task usability. We present an Adaptive Depth Sparse Framework (AdaDSF) that converts off-the-shelf pre-trained LLMs into depth-sparse models without full retraining. Our key insight is that layers contribute unequally to representation transformation, characterized by the cosine similarity between layer input and output hidden states. Based on this, AdaDSF assigns layer-wise token retention ratios from similarity statistics, uses a lightweight router to select informative tokens at each layer, and introduces a feature-preserving alignment objective to match intermediate and final representations between sparse and dense models. On GPT-NeoX and Qwen2.5 over language modeling and commonsense reasoning, AdaDSF substantially reduces inference FLOPs while preserving performance close to dense counterparts. Under comparable sparsity, AdaDSF consistently yields smaller accuracy degradation than strong baselines including MoD, D-LLM, and DLO.

</details>


### [157] [GRADRAG: Cross-Component Prompt Adaptation for Coordinated Multi-Agent RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21324)
*Paolo Pedinotti,Enrico Santus*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GRADRAG是一个通过将RAG流水线建模为计算图并传播结构化评估反馈来协调多智能体改进的框架，在SQUALITY和QMSUM基准上相比单步优化基线实现了12-15个百分点的净偏好提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统虽然使用多LLM智能体，但大多孤立优化各组件，缺乏跨组件的协调改进机制。

Method: 将RAG流水线建模为计算图，通过Evaluator批判下游答案和支持证据产生可操作反馈，Prompt Optimizer利用反馈迭代更新检索器、图构建器和回答器等上游智能体，并在输出满意时触发早停。

Result: 在SQUALITY和QMSUM基准上，在平面块检索和图检索两种范式下，GRADRAG均优于仅更新最终生成器的单步优化基线，LLM评判的成对比较中达到12-15个百分点的净偏好优势，且大多数增益在两次迭代内实现。

Conclusion: 跨组件的提示自适应优化比仅优化最终生成器更有效，且该框架具有高效性（早停机制）和通用性（适用于不同检索范式）。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems increasingly employ multiple LLM agents. Yet, most prior work optimizes components in isolation rather than coordinating improvements across the pipeline. We introduce GRADRAG, a framework for cross-component prompt adaptation that models the RAG pipeline as a computational graph and propagates structured evaluation feedback to update upstream agents. An Evaluator critiques downstream answers and supporting evidence, producing actionable feedback that a Prompt Optimizer uses to iteratively update adaptive agents, such as retrievers, graph constructors, and answerers. The Evaluator also triggers early stopping when the output is deemed satisfactory. We evaluate GRADRAG on the SQUALITY and QMSUM benchmarks under two retrieval paradigms: flat chunk-based retrieval using IRCoT-style query refinement (Trivedi et al., 2023), and graph-based retrieval that constructs and iteratively enriches an entity-relation graph from the document. Across both settings, GRADRAG consistently outperforms one-step refinement baselines that update only the final generator, achieving a 12-15 percentage point net preference margin in LLM-judged pairwise comparisons, with most gains realized within two refinement iterations.

</details>


### [158] [Phonetic forced alignment for low-resource language varieties: Model training and evaluation on Chengdu Mandarin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21332)
*Zhiheng Qian,Aini Li,Hai Hu,Liang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对成都官话（一种低资源语言变体）训练了两种语音强制对齐模型（文本依赖型GMM-HMM和文本无关型帧分类模型），在专家标注测试集上显著优于标准普通话基线，分别将音素边界差异降低了31.8%和61.2%，建立了一条无需人工标注的低资源语言对齐器开发流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音强制对齐系统缺乏针对低资源语言变体的专用模型，手动标注耗时费力，亟需一种高效的自动化方案。

Method: 使用17小时的成都官话语料和自定义G2P词典，训练了文本依赖型GMM-HMM模型（Chengdu-MFA），并将其伪标签用于微调预训练音频编码器，实现文本无关的对齐（Chengdu-FC）。

Result: 在专家标注测试集上，Chengdu-MFA将音素边界差异降低31.8%，Chengdu-FC降低61.2%，均显著优于标准普通话基线。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一条实用的自举流水线，能够为低资源语言变体开发高精度对齐器，避免了耗时的人工标注过程。

Abstract: Phonetic forced alignment is a key technique in phonetic research, yet existing alignment systems lack specialized models for low-resource language varieties. We address this by training text-dependent and text-independent aligners for Chengdu Mandarin using a 17-hour corpus and a custom G2P dictionary. We trained a text-dependent GMM-HMM model (Chengdu-MFA) and fine-tuned a pretrained audio encoder on frame classification with Chengdu-MFA's pseudo label for text-independent alignment (Chengdu-FC). Evaluation on an expert-annotated test set show that both methods significantly outperform Standard Mandarin baselines. Chengdu-MFA reduced average phone boundary differences by 31.8%, while Chengdu-FC achieved a 61.2% reduction. This work establishes a practical bootstrapping pipeline for developing accurate aligners for under-resourced varieties without labor- and time-intensive manual annotation.

</details>


### [159] [Capital Markets LLM Reliability Score (CM-LRS): From Plausible to Bankable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21340)
*Prerit Ahuja*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CM-LRS评分体系，从七个维度评估大模型在资本市场工作流中的"可银行性"（而非仅流畅性）。闭源模型表现接近（4.09-4.31分），远超开源模型（3.15分），差距集中在检索与综合而非提取环节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估关注问答层准确性或表面流畅性，但资本市场工作流需要的是"可辩护性"（bankability），即面对交易对手或监管机构时能用文档证成的输出质量，现有基准无法覆盖这一差距。

Method: 设计CM-LRS评分体系，覆盖7个维度（事实准确性、证据可追溯性、数值一致性、工作流完整性、来源纪律、决策有用性、可审计性），每维0-5分，可调权。在5个工作流上对4个模型（含闭源和开源）由4个独立LLM裁判打分。

Result: 1) 闭源模型差距极小（Sonnet 4.31 vs Opus 4.30 vs GPT 4.09），开源基准Llama仅3.15；2) 差距集中在检索（2.23分差）和综合（2.15），而非提取（0.84）；3) 决策有用性维度跨模型差异最大（发行人画像达4分差），裁判间一致性最高（r=0.52）。

Conclusion: 在资本市场场景中，"合理性"廉价，"可银行性"才是真正的准入门槛。CM-LRS为这一专业评估提供了可操作框架。

Abstract: In capital-markets workflows the question is rarely whether a large language model can produce a fluent draft, but whether the draft is bankable: defensible in front of a counter-party or a regulator, with the documents in hand. Existing methods address parts of that gap: open-domain QA benchmarks reward surface accuracy, and finance benchmarks (FinanceBench, FinQA, ConvFinQA) advance document-grounded and numerical QA but evaluate at the question-answer layer rather than the workflow outputs practitioners defend.
  We introduce CM-LRS, a Capital Markets LLM Reliability Score, evaluating outputs at the workflow-output layer across seven dimensions: factual accuracy, evidence traceability, numerical consistency, workflow completeness, source discipline, decision usefulness, and reviewability/auditability. Each is scored 0-5 against a rubric anchored on signals reviewers in regulated settings use; the aggregate is tunable to the workflow.
  We demonstrate CM-LRS on five workflows (DCM transaction-terms extraction, precedent retrieval, issuer profile synthesis, M&A transaction-comparable reasoning, ECM transaction-terms extraction) over public SEC EDGAR filings, a public UK takeover release, and fictional synthetic supplements, scoring four models against four independent LLM judges spanning three model families.
  Three findings. First, the frontier closed-source models cluster within 0.22 points on four-judge averaged CM-LRS (Sonnet 4.6 = 4.31, Opus 4.7 = 4.30, GPT-5.5 = 4.09); all four judges place the open-weights baseline (Llama 3.3 70B = 3.15) last. Second, that gap concentrates on retrieval (2.23) and synthesis (2.15), not extraction (0.84). Third, Decision Usefulness shows the widest cross-model dispersion of any dimension (4.0 points on issuer profiling) and top-tier inter-judge agreement (mean r = 0.52).
  Plausibility is cheap. Bankability is the bar.

</details>


### [160] [Word meaning co-determines vowel-inherent spectral change. A corpus-based investigation of conversational Mandarin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21391)
*Xiaoyun Jin,Mirjam Ernestus,R. Harald Baayen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨自发性对话普通话中元音内在频谱变化，使用广义加性模型和分布语义词嵌入，发现当控制多种变量后，元音共振峰轨迹具有与语境中词义相关的词特异性成分，表明词义共同决定了发音的精细细节，挑战了模块化认知模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统认知认为语音产出是模块化的，语义与发音精细细节相互独立。本研究旨在检验词义是否影响元音声学实现的微观动态变化。

Method: 使用广义加性模型（GAM）分析自发性对话普通话中元音的F1、F2轨迹变化，结合分布语义学的词嵌入（contextualized embeddings），控制时长、性别、发音人、协同发音、元音身份和话语位置等变量。

Result: 元音的F1和F2轨迹可从语境化词嵌入中预测，预测精度显著高于排列基线（permutation baseline）。

Conclusion: 词义与发音精细细节之间存在非模块化的协同关系，语义信息在语音产出过程中影响元音的动态声学实现。

Abstract: This study investigates vowel-inherent spectral change (VISC) in spontaneous conversational Mandarin. Using the generalized additive model and word embeddings from distributional semantics, we show that, when controlling for variables such as vowel duration, gender, speaker identity, co-articulation, vowel identity, and utterance position, vowel formant trajectory dynamics have word-specific components that are tied to their meaning in context: The F1 and F2 trajectories of words can be predicted from their contextualized embeddings with an accuracy that substantially exceeds a permutation baseline. Challenging modular cognitive models of speech production, these results indicate that, words' semantics co-determine the fine details of their articulation.

</details>


### [161] [MemTools: A Unified Research Framework for Interoperable Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21404)
*Chengfeng Zhao,Jinhui Chen,Sirui Liang,Shizhu He,Yequan Wang,Jun Zhao,Kang Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MemTools是一个用于智能体记忆系统的互操作性研究框架，通过解耦记忆组件与部署环境、标准化记忆生命周期、分离基准数据集与执行协议，以及提供统一计算接口支持符号、神经和多模态记忆表征的协调，实现了记忆设计变量的系统隔离与分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体记忆系统存在架构碎片化问题：实现常耦合记忆生命周期的不同阶段、评估逻辑与特定数据集纠缠、对异构记忆类型管理支持有限，阻碍了系统性研究。

Method: 通过声明式数据契约标准化记忆生命周期实现组件互换组装；正交分离基准数据集与执行协议以支持可控评估；提供统一计算接口在共享运行时中协调符号、神经和多模态记忆表征。

Result: 在跨系统组件集成、评估协议重配置和异构记忆协调等实证评估中，MemTools能够实现记忆设计变量的系统隔离与分析。

Conclusion: MemTools为推进智能体记忆的 principled research（原则性研究）提供了一个实用且可扩展的基础设施。

Abstract: While memory systems are essential for agent architectures, pervasive architectural fragmentation restricts systematic research. Existing implementations typically couple different stages of the memory lifecycle, entangle evaluation logic with specific datasets, and provide limited support for the management of heterogeneous memory types. We introduce MemTools, an interoperability research framework that decouples memory system components from their underlying deployment environments. MemTools standardizes the memory lifecycle through declarative data contracts, enabling the interchangeable assembly of components across different systems. It orthogonally separates benchmark datasets from execution protocols to facilitate controlled assessments. Furthermore, MemTools provides a unified computational interface for coordinating symbolic, neural, and multimodal memory representations within a shared runtime. Empirical evaluations on cross-system component integration, evaluation protocol reconfiguration, and heterogeneous memory coordination demonstrate that MemTools enables systematic isolation and analysis of memory design variables. These findings suggest that MemTools provides a practical and extensible infrastructure for advancing principled research on agent memory.

</details>


### [162] [Anti-Periodic Positional Encoding: Möbius Boundary Conditions Make In-Context Retrieval Reliable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21405)
*Ji Ho Bae*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Möbius RoPE，一种基于反周期频率梯度的旋转位置编码，使序列两端通过狄利克雷“偶极子”确定性耦合，在保持困惑度的同时显著提升长上下文检索的可靠性（90.3% vs 63.3%），且可通过一行代码替换实现零成本保险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有旋转位置编码（RoPE）在长上下文检索中存在不稳定性问题，检索表现方差大（如63.3±31.4%），亟需一种能提供确定性长程位置耦合的编码方法。

Method: 设计反周期频率梯θ_i=π(2i+1)/N，使每个旋转平面在训练上下文中推进奇数倍π，产生位置holonomy为-1，形成闭合形式狄利克雷“偶极子”边界条件；实验在160M和410M参数模型上预训练48个模型，仅替换25%注意力头的频率。

Result: 混合模型困惑度几乎不变（29.66 vs 29.72），但单针检索准确率从63.3±31.4%提升至90.3±5.7%（p<0.05）；周期性或非周期性频率梯无法复现效果；将训练好的模型频率换回标准RoPE后检索性能崩溃；无位置编码模型困惑度增加13%。

Conclusion: Möbius RoPE通过反周期边界条件实现了长上下文检索的可靠性与困惑度保持之间的最优平衡，提供了一种零成本的检索稳定性保险方案，但效果限于训练窗口内的单针检索。

Abstract: Möbius RoPE is a rotary positional encoding built on the anti-periodic frequency ladder $θ_i=π(2i+1)/N$: every rotation plane advances by an odd multiple of $π$ across the training context, so the positional holonomy is $-1$ and the two ends of the sequence are deterministically coupled through a closed-form Dirichlet "dipole"; to our knowledge this is the first anti-periodic boundary condition in positional encoding. We verify the theory numerically to $\sim 10^{-6}$ and pretrain 48 models spanning six 160M-class and three 410M-class arms (2B FineWeb-Edu tokens each; the hybrid arm puts Möbius frequencies on 25% of heads). Hybrid perplexity is unchanged (29.66 vs. 29.72), but needle-in-a-haystack retrieval becomes reliable: $90.3\pm5.7\%$ versus $63.3\pm31.4\%$ at context 512 ($n=6$ seeds), observed worst seed 86% versus 14%, robust variance tests $p=0.013$-$0.029$ (unadjusted), recurring at 410M (Levene $p=0.040$). Matched controls isolate the mechanism: an aperiodic ladder in the same frequency band reproduces none of the effect, and a periodic (holonomy $+1$) ladder only a fraction. Swapping trained models' frequency table back to standard RoPE (weights frozen) collapses retrieval, with damage concentrated on far needles: trained models depend on this long-range geometry. A NoPE arm is even more reliable at short context but pays a 13% perplexity tax and extrapolates worst; only the anti-periodic hybrid pairs baseline perplexity with a high reliability floor. The effect is scoped to single-needle retrieval within the training window; a one-line frequency swap thus provides zero-cost insurance against the retrieval seed lottery.

</details>


### [163] [An Evaluation Framework for Structured Audio Captions Validated by Controlled Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21424)
*Liang-Yuan Wu,Sripathi Sridhar,Mark Cartwright,Magdalena Fuentes*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一个针对结构化音频描述的多轴评估框架，结合LLM判断和确定性计算指标，并通过受控扰动测试验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频字幕指标仅评估扁平文本输出，无法可靠评估结构化音频描述中的多模态属性。

Method: 基于AudioCards数据集，在标签集、描述、逻辑推理、数值测量和频谱轮廓五个正交轴上评估；结合LLM裁判（捕捉语义细节）和确定性计算指标（精确测量声学偏差）；引入受控扰动测试协议注入类型化、分级错误来验证框架可靠性。

Result: 该框架成功区分了保留语义的改写与真实的语义/声学损坏。

Conclusion: 提出的多轴评估框架能有效评估结构化音频描述，弥补了现有指标在评估结构化、多模态音频数据方面的不足。

Abstract: Recent advancements in automated audio captioning (AAC) have shifted from monolithic sentence generation toward structured formats that explicitly disentangle distinct acoustic and semantic properties. However, evaluating this heterogeneous data remains a significant challenge. Existing caption metrics focus on flat textual outputs and fail to reliably assess multimodal attributes. To bridge this gap, we propose a multi-axis evaluation framework tailored for structured audio descriptions. Building on the AudioCards dataset, we evaluate outputs across five orthogonal axes: tag-sets, descriptions, logical reasoning, numeric measurements, and spectral profiles. Our approach combines Large Language Model (LLM) judges to capture semantic nuance with deterministic computational metrics to precisely measure acoustic deviations. To rigorously validate the reliability of this framework, we introduce a controlled perturbation testing protocol that injects typed, graded errors into groundtruth annotations. Our results demonstrate that this framework successfully distinguishes meaning-preserving paraphrases from genuine semantic and acoustic corruptions.

</details>


### [164] [Token Budget Saturation and Mechanistic Early Detection of Reasoning Non-Convergence in Chain-of-Thought Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21433)
*Renuka Oladri,Niveda Jawahar,Abdirisak Mohamed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B等链式思考模型呈现出双峰收敛模式：在预算内终止的生成准确率90.3%，耗尽预算的仅6.6%（收敛率62.0%）。通过第20层第150个token的隐藏状态线性探针可早期预测收敛结果（AUC 0.608），为早期退出和自适应计算分配提供可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 链式思考推理模型存在显著的"收敛-非收敛"双峰现象，非收敛生成几乎完全失败（准确率仅6.6%），若能提前检测到这种失败倾向，可避免浪费计算资源。

Method: 在token位置50-300的隐藏状态上训练线性探针，使用第20层激活值进行收敛预测，并与基于token熵和重复统计的行为基线对比，通过5折交叉验证和置换检验评估。

Result: 第20层第150个token的激活值AUC达0.608（±0.080），token 50处即可高于随机水平。激活探针始终优于行为基线。置换检验p=0.063，未达常规显著性阈值。

Conclusion: 收敛命运在中间表征中部分编码，可早期检测，为早期退出推理和自适应计算分配开辟道路。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought reasoning models such as DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B exhibit a bimodal convergence pattern: generations either terminate within a token budget (converged) or exhaust it without reaching a conclusion (non-converged). We characterize this phenomenon empirically, showing that converged generations achieve 90.3% accuracy on AIME 1983-2024 while non-converged ones achieve only 6.6%, with an overall convergence rate of 62.0%. We then ask whether this outcome is detectable early in the thinking chain using internal model representations. Training linear probes on hidden-state activations at token positions 50-300, we find that layer-20 activations at token 150 achieve AUC 0.608 (+-0.080, 5-fold CV), reliably above chance even at token 50. Activation probes consistently outperform behavioral baselines derived from token entropy and repetition statistics. A sweep-level permutation test yields p=0.063 (100,000 permutations), consistent with a modest signal that our sample size cannot confirm at conventional thresholds. These findings suggest that convergence fate is partially encoded in intermediate representations well before the generation ends, opening a path toward early-exit inference and adaptive compute allocation.

</details>


### [165] [When Trivia Is Not Trivial: Everyday Knowledge Failures in Multilingual LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21445)
*Anna Mosolova,Djamé Seddah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了多语言问答基准TriviaRoomQA，评估30个开源大语言模型在288个主题（常识与文化长尾知识）上的表现。模型在历史、地理等知识密集型领域表现强，但在名人、音乐等流行文化领域偏弱，且知识可及性存在语言依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学术基准趋于饱和，且忽略了对日常文化、长尾知识的评估。作者希望填补这一空白，测试LLM在真实问答场景（如问答比赛）中的表现。

Method: 构建TriviaRoomQA基准，包含3300道平行多选问题（6种欧洲语言）及5340道法语专属问题，覆盖288个主题。评测30个7B-70B参数的开源LLM。

Result: 模型在历史、地理、数学等知识密集型主题上表现好，在流行文化主题（名人、音乐、电影、新闻）上表现弱；相同问题下不同语言的表现存在差异，知识访问并非语言无关。

Conclusion: 现有LLM在文化与常识性知识上存在显著缺口，当前学术基准无法捕捉这一问题；知识存储与语言之间存在依赖关系。

Abstract: Quiz rooms, trivia nights, and quiz shows challenge human knowledge across a wide range of topics, from canonical facts to everyday culture. In this paper, we examine whether large language models (LLMs) can perform competitively in such settings, using quiz-style questions to test them on both common and niche topics. We introduce TriviaRoomQA, a multilingual benchmark designed to evaluate everyday, culturally grounded, and long-tail knowledge across 288 topics. The benchmark contains 3,300 parallel multiple-choice questions in six European languages and additional 5,340 French-only questions for a more fine-grained case study. We evaluate 30 open-weight LLMs from European, Asian, and North American providers, covering models from 7 to 70B parameters. We find that models are strong on knowledge-intensive topics such as history, geography, and mathematics, but substantially weaker on everyday popular-culture topics such as celebrities, music, movies, and news. Moreover, model performance varies across languages even for the same underlying questions, suggesting that access to factual knowledge is not always language-independent. In sum, our dataset and experiments demonstrate an important knowledge gap which is not captured by existing academic-based saturated benchmarks.

</details>


### [166] [RUMBA: Russian User Memory Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21447)
*Elizaveta Shevtsova,Inna Glebkina,Mark Baushenko,Pavel Gulyaev,Alena Fenogenova*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RUMBA是一个面向俄语长时对话记忆的细粒度基准测试，包含时间戳对话和QA对，用于评估模型在跨会话检索、组合和推理方面的能力，同时提供对齐的英文子集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM长时记忆基准以英语为中心，仅依赖聚合检索指标，无法捕捉长程上下文、时间信息和推理之间的交互，亟需更精细的评估方法。

Method: 构建了带时间戳的用户-助手对话数据集，设计了细粒度的记忆问题类型分类体系，统一考虑了语义类型、会话范围、时间推理和时间表达明确性四个维度。

Result: 通过评估现有记忆系统和长上下文模型，展示了RUMBA作为诊断工具的能力，能够分析模型在不同基准切片上的行为，识别不同记忆机制的优点和失败模式。

Conclusion: RUMBA填补了非英语长时对话记忆评估的空白，其细粒度分类学为理解模型记忆能力提供了更深入的诊断视角。

Abstract: The ability to handle long-term memory in LLMs is becoming increasingly critical, yet existing benchmarks remain English-centric and rely on aggregate retrieval metrics, failing to capture interactions between long-range context, temporal information, and reasoning. To address this, we introduce RUMBA (Russian User Memory BenchmArk) - a new benchmark for long-term conversational memory that provides a fine-grained taxonomy of memory-centric question types and a unified methodology accounting for semantic type, session scope, temporal reasoning, and the explicitness of temporal expressions. RUMBA consists of timestamped user-assistant dialogues with QA pairs requiring retrieval, combination, and reasoning across sessions. While designed for Russian, we also provide an aligned English subset under the same methodology. We evaluate contemporary memory systems and long-context models, and show how RUMBA serves as a diagnostic tool to analyze model behavior across benchmark slices and identify strengths and failure modes of different memory mechanisms.

</details>


### [167] [What, Where, and How: Disentangling the Roles of Task, Language, and Model in Code Model Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21491)
*Piotr Wilam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在2×2设计（Python/Rust × Qwen/DeepSeek）中，系统研究了代码语言模型的概念电路通用性：任务决定哪些概念有电路（What），模型决定电路位置和增长模式（Where/How），通用性只存在于表征内容而非计算组织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究独立训练的代码语言模型在表征语法概念时是否具有通用性，以及这种通用性在任务、语言和模型三个维度上的分布情况。

Method: 扩展概念电路提取方法至2×2设计（两种编程语言×两种模型），在全部四个单元格中相同地测量了完整的语法概念清单（Python 58个，Rust 57个），结合消融实验和线性探针验证电路的功能性。

Result: 任务决定哪些概念被分配电路（Spearman ρ≈0.64-0.67）；模型决定电路位置（Qwen在L17-19，DeepSeek在L6-7）和增长模式（Qwen原子概念有早期峰值）；Rust电路密度是Python的2-3倍；两模型间存在神经元共享；Qwen将Rust的9个类型特征关键词绑定为紧密神经元簇。

Conclusion: 电路通用性不是单一答案：表征内容（What）通用，计算组织（Where/How）不通用。通用性是表征内容的属性，而非计算组织的属性。

Abstract: Do independently trained language models come to represent the same thing in the same way? We answer for code, extending a recently introduced concept-circuit extraction method to a 2x2 design -- Python and Rust crossed with Qwen2.5-Coder-7B and DeepSeek-Coder-V1-6.7B -- and measuring a complete inventory of grammatical concepts (58 Python, 57 Rust) identically in all four cells: the smallest design that separates what depends on the task, the language, and the model.
  The answer splits into three parts. What earns dedicated circuitry is set by the task: the models agree on which concepts receive circuits (Spearman $ρ$ = 0.638 for Python, 0.673 for Rust, both p < $10^{-7}$). Where those circuits sit is set by the model: Qwen processes concepts in a late band (~L17-19), DeepSeek at L6-7, for both languages. How circuits grow across layers is also set by the model: Qwen gives its atomic concepts an early spike that DeepSeek does not. "Are circuits universal?" thus has no single answer: yes for What, no for Where and How -- universality is a property of representational content, not of computational organisation.
  None of this structure was fixed in advance. The agreement could have landed anywhere between independence and identity; it lands at $ρ\approx 0.65$. Rust constructs receive 2-3x more concept-specific circuitry than their Python equivalents, in both models. Both models share neurons between the languages (6/7 and 7/7 paired constructs), DeepSeek 1.94x more than Qwen -- a direction no prior result predicts. And Qwen binds nine keywords of Rust's type-and-trait machinery into one tight neuron cluster (Jaccard 0.535 vs null 0.112, p < 0.001), a semantic dimension invisible in surface syntax. Ablation and linear probes confirm the circuits are functional.
  All claims are scoped to this 2x2; whether the per-model profile predicts a third model is the designed next test.

</details>


### [168] [Artificial Epanorthosis: Why large language models overuse a classical rhetorical figure, and how to mitigate it](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21498)
*Federico Boggia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 这篇论文发现大型语言模型中系统性地过度使用“自我纠正（epanorthosis）”这一修辞手法，认为其主要源于训练数据中的促销性文本和偏好调优（RLHF），而非自回归生成机制。通过Epanorthosis Index测量发现模型在不同语体中出现校准偏差：在演说中过度使用，在非正式问答中使用不足。论文提出了LoRA适配器、指令微调等缓解方案，并主张目标应是校准到人类使用的自然频率，而非完全消除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示大语言模型中一种特定修辞手法（epanorthosis）的系统性过度使用现象，并探究其根本原因——不是生成机制本身，而是训练数据分布和RLHF偏好调优所塑造的"训练习惯"。

Method: 构建Epanorthosis Index（密度比率指标），对三种规模、多种语体（演说、问答、论述、新闻、百科）的指令调优模型进行测量，并与人类基线对比；探索基于LoRA适配器、单行指令、SFT微调等缓解技术的效果。

Result: 模型在演说语体中过度使用（约2倍，意大利语中近3倍，且规模越大越严重），在非正式问答中反而使用不足；而论述、新闻、百科语体中与人类表现匹配。单行指令可减少一半至近四分之三，SFT适配器几乎可完全消除该修辞手法。

Conclusion: 目标不是消除模型中的修辞手法，而是校准到各语体的人类使用频率。真正的风险不是机器像人一样写作，而是人类开始像机器一样写作。

Abstract: A rhetorical figure that Cicero and Quintilian catalogued two thousand years ago reappears, systematically, in the text of large language models: epanorthosis, the self-correction of the specimen «This is not a course. It is a journey of transformation». This essay argues that the overuse is a trained disposition, driven mainly by a training distribution rich in promotional prose and by preference tuning (RLHF) that rewards confident, emphatic phrasing; the left-to-right nature of generation is an amplifier rather than the root cause. Building on evidence that models diverge from human rhetorical style, and on Fontanier's classification of epanorthosis as a figure of thought, it sets out a programme that scores the figure against genre-specific human baselines through an Epanorthosis Index (density relative to the human rate). A first measurement, on three sizes of one instruction-tuned model family, finds mis-calibration by register in both directions: the models overshoot in oratory (about twofold, near threefold in Italian, concentrated in the larger tiers) and undershoot in informal question-and-answer writing, while matching humans in argument, journalism, and encyclopedic prose. Three constructive contributions follow: a survey of mitigation techniques centred on lightweight LoRA adapters; a demonstration, in Italian, that a one-line instruction cuts the figure by half to nearly three-quarters and that a supervised-fine-tuning adapter removes it almost entirely, with a scaling coefficient that dials the reduction back onto the human rate; and the argument that the target is calibration to the human rate for each genre, not elimination. It closes on the stakes: the real risk is that we begin to write like the machines.

</details>


### [169] [DONDO: Open w2v-BERT Speech-Recognition Base Models for African Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21540)
*Paul Azunre*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DONDO是一个面向非洲语言的开放ASR模型家族，基于w2v-BERT 2.0，包含21个单语和5个多语模型，覆盖27种语言变体。通过两步/三步学习率退火微调策略和轻量级语言条件机制，多语模型在词错误率上达到10-13%，接近单语模型性能，所有模型以Apache-2.0许可发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非洲语言缺乏足够的转录音频数据，现有ASR模型覆盖不足。作者旨在构建开放、许可宽松的ASR基础模型，覆盖多种非洲语言，解决数据匮乏问题，同时实现单检查点多语言覆盖。

Method: 基于w2v-BERT 2.0自监督语音编码器，采用两步（或三步）学习率退火微调：先以高学习率适配共享多语模型，再退火恢复甚至超越单语基线。引入轻量级语言条件机制，将one-hot语言身份作为前缀帧拼接到声学特征前，实现推理时目标语言引导。

Result: 五组多语模型家族平均词错误率（WER）达10-13%，与单语模型差距大幅缩小。覆盖语言的第一语言使用者约一亿人，若计入第二语言则更多。所有模型以Apache-2.0许可在Hugging Face发布。

Conclusion: DONDO成功证明了在低资源非洲语言上，通过精心设计的微调策略和语言条件机制，多语模型能够接近单语模型性能，为非洲语言ASR提供了开放、可商用的基础资源，并支持社区进一步微调。

Abstract: We present DONDO, a family of open, permissively licensed automatic speech recognition (ASR) base models for African languages, built on the w2v-BERT 2.0 self-supervised speech encoder. DONDO comprises twenty-one monolingual models and five multilingual models spanning twenty-seven language varieties across Ghana, Sierra Leone, Nigeria, Senegal, Kenya and Zimbabwe. Models are fine-tuned primarily on read speech drawn from religious texts, which offer broad, license-clear and orthographically consistent coverage for languages that otherwise lack transcribed audio. We describe a two-step (and, for one family, three-step) learning-rate-annealed fine-tuning procedure that first adapts a shared multilingual model at a high learning rate and then anneals it to recover, and in several cases surpass, strong monolingual baselines. We further describe a lightweight language-conditioning mechanism that injects a one-hot language identity as a sequence of prefix frames prepended to the acoustic features, allowing a single multilingual checkpoint to be steered to a target language at inference. Across the five multilingual families the annealed models reach average word error rates (WER) of 10-13%, closing most of the gap to monolingual models while covering many languages in a single checkpoint. All models are released on the Hugging Face KhayaAI organisation under the Apache-2.0 license (attribution only) so that others may fine-tune them freely, including for commercial use. We provide a conservative estimate that the languages covered are spoken by on the order of one hundred million first-language speakers, and by substantially more when second-language use is included.

</details>


### [170] [MedGame: Storytelling Gamification Empowered by Large Language Models for Medical Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21570)
*Qian Wu,Xinrong Zhou,Zizhan Ma,Kai Chen,Zheyao Gao,Xun Lin,Hongqiu Wu,Longfei Gou,Yixiao Liu,Ann Sin Nga Lau,Qi Dou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MedGame将静态临床病例转化为结构化、可执行的叙事游戏，采用双引擎设计（医学叙事设计器+故事导演），通过5000例基准测试和用户研究验证了其比纯文本方案更吸引学习者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM医学教育系统多局限于问答或单轮反馈，缺乏将完整临床案例组织为以决策为中心的学习轨迹的能力。

Method: 提出MedGame框架：(1) 双引擎设计——Medical Narrative Designer生成病例驱动的临床故事线与决策节点，Story Director转化为依赖感知的多模态编排方案；(2) 构建MedGame Bench基准（5000例）用于评估；(3) 对开源LLM进行任务特定微调。

Result: 任务特定微调显著提升开源LLM在MedGame Bench上的表现，缩小了与商业模型的差距；学生试点研究发现MedGame比纯文本方案更具吸引力和实用性。

Conclusion: MedGame通过游戏化叙事框架有效提升了医学教育中的学习者参与度，并且开源模型通过微调可达到接近商业模型的水平。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) show promise for medical education, but most existing systems focus on localized interactions such as question answering or single-turn feedback, rather than organizing an entire clinical case into a decision-centered learning trajectory. We introduce \textit{MedGame}, a framework that transforms static clinical cases into structured, executable storytelling games. MedGame uses a dual-engine design: a Medical Narrative Designer synthesizes case-grounded clinical storylines with states and decision nodes, while a Story Director converts them into dependency-aware multimodal orchestration plans rendered by our released interactive platform. We construct MedGame Bench, a 5,000-case benchmark and evaluation protocol for Medical Narrative Generation and Story Direction. Experiments show that task-specific fine-tuning substantially improves open-source LLMs on MedGame Bench and narrows the gap with commercial models. A pilot student study further shows that learners perceive MedGame as more engaging and useful than text-only alternatives.

</details>


### [171] [Surprisal Theory is Tautological (without Rational Grounding)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21574)
*Ryan Cotterell*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文论证了惊奇理论（Surprisal Theory）中"处理难度与语言模型惊奇度呈仿射关系"的命题是一个套套逻辑（tautology），因为在温和技术条件下，任何非负难度度量都存在对应的语言模型使其惊奇度与之仿射相关。过去二十年隐含的假设——语言模型应为训练语料的生成分布——已被近期实证推翻（更好的语料模型反而预测更差）。要打破套套逻辑，必须引入理性主义干预，从记忆约束或处理目标等非经验动机推导语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示惊奇理论中隐含的套套逻辑问题，指出其看似可证伪实则不可证伪的本质，并挑战了过去二十年心理语言学中"语料拟合度越好则预测人类行为越准"的隐含假设。

Method: 通过形式化论证（任何非负难度度量在温和条件下总能找到对应语言模型使其惊奇度与之仿射相关）和实证文献回顾（更好的语料模型反而预测更差），从理论分析和经验证据两个层面揭示套套逻辑。

Result: 1. 惊奇理论若无额外约束是一个套套逻辑，任何难度模式都可被某种语言模型解释。2. 语料生成分布的隐含假设已被打破——语料拟合度与行为预测准确性之间存在背离。3. 需要理性主义干预才能打破套套逻辑。

Conclusion: 打破套套逻辑的唯一路径是放弃纯经验主义的语言模型选择，转向基于理性主义（如记忆约束、处理目标）构建语言模型，使其独立于所要解释的行为数据本身。

Abstract: Surprisal theory holds that the human processing difficulty of a linguistic unit in context is an affine function of its surprisal under some language model. I argue this claim is a tautology without further constraint: for any non-negative difficulty measure over units in context, there exists a language model whose surprisal is an affine function of it under mild technical conditions. Therefore, because any pattern of difficulty is consistent with some language model, without an additional constraint on the language model, surprisal theory makes no falsifiable predictions. The tautology was long obscured by an assumption implicit in two decades of psycholinguistic work---that the relevant language model is the distribution that generated the training corpus, so that improving corpus fit improves predictions of human behavior. Recent empirical work has undermined this assumption, demonstrating that better corpus models can be worse predictors of processing difficulty. I conclude that breaking the tautology requires a rationalist intervention, i.e., the relevant language model must be derived from a non-empirically motivated model of the comprehender, which could be based on, for instance, memory constraints or processing goals, and that, thus, does not depend on the behavioral data surprisal theory is meant to explain.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [172] [AINTMA: Agentic AI Architecture for Autonomous Test Management with Generative Intelligence, Secure Cloud Communication and Adaptive Quality Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20452)
*Vinil Pasupuleti,Shyalendar Reddy Allala,Siva Rama Krishna Varma Bayyavarapu,Shrey Tyagi,Srinivasateja Songa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AINTMA，一个基于多智能体AI的自主测试管理架构，包含六个专用AI智能体，在18个月12个项目的评估中实现了88.4%的测试优先级排序准确率、43%的测试周期缩减、缺陷逃逸率从8.3%降至2.1%，以及340%的ROI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代软件质量保证需要智能、自主的系统，能够在分布式云环境中进行自适应决策，而传统测试管理方法难以满足这一需求。

Method: 设计了一个六智能体系统（测试发现、风险评估、强化学习优先级排序、执行编排、生成式质量智能、云安全监控），通过零信任API网关和加密通信协调，其中RL优先级排序将测试选择建模为马尔可夫决策过程，生成式质量智能使用大语言模型产出自然语言质量报告。

Result: 88.4%测试优先级排序准确率（APFD，对比随机51.2%、最佳商业基线82.1%）；43%测试周期缩减；缺陷逃逸率从8.3%降至2.1%；340% ROI，9个月回本；支持50,000+测试用例，响应时间<400ms；生成式智能模块开发者有用性评分4.3/5.0。

Conclusion: 智能体AI（结合自主多智能体协调、生成式智能和安全的智能连接）能够从根本上推进云规模企业环境中的软件质量管理。

Abstract: Modern software quality assurance demands intelligent, autonomous systems capable of adaptive decision-making across distributed cloud environments. This paper presents AINTMA (Agentic Intelligent Test Management Architecture), a multi-agent agentic AI system that transforms traditional test management into an autonomous quality intelligence ecosystem. AINTMA deploys six specialized AI agents (Test Discovery, Risk Assessment, Reinforcement Learning Prioritization, Execution Orchestration, Generative Quality Intelligence, and Cloud Security Monitor) coordinated through a secure multi-agent communication framework over a cloud-native microservices infrastructure. The Generative Quality Intelligence agent employs large language models to produce plain language quality narratives, defect risk summaries, and data-augmented test recommendations. The RL Prioritization agent models test selection as a Markov Decision Process, learning contextual policies from large-scale historical test execution data (47 features, rolling 36-month window). Secure cloud communication is enforced through a zero-trust API gateway with OAuth2/JWT authentication, encrypted inter-agent messaging, and multi-tenant isolation. Evaluation across 12 heterogeneous software projects over 18 months demonstrates: 88.4% test prioritization accuracy (APFD, vs. 51.2% random, 82.1% best commercial baseline); 43% test cycle time reduction; defect escape rate reduced from 8.3% to 2.1%; 340% ROI at 9-month payback. The agentic architecture scales to 50,000+ test cases with sub-400ms response time, and the generative intelligence module achieves 4.3/5.0 developer usefulness rating. AINTMA demonstrates that agentic AI, combining autonomous multi-agent coordination, generative intelligence and secure smart connectivity, can fundamentally advance software quality management in cloud-scale enterprise environments.

</details>


### [173] [Marking the Wrong Symptoms: Evaluating LLM Watermarks in Medical Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20462)
*Melanie Rieff,Robin Staab,Thibaud Gloaguen,Stefan Hegselmann,Martin Vechev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了LLM水印对医学性能的影响，发现水印会导致词汇损坏、术语幻觉、图像发现错误归因/遗漏等问题，并指出现有通用基准无法检测这些临床关键缺陷，呼吁医学领域必须进行领域特异性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地应用于临床工作流，需要可靠的水印技术来追踪模型输出，但现有水印评估仅在通用基准上进行，忽略了医学领域中微小token扰动可能导致重大语义变化的问题。

Method: 在11个LLM和7个VLM上基准测试5种水印方案，涵盖单模态和多模态临床推理任务，并引入经人类专家验证的流水线系统评估医学推理质量、术语精确性和幻觉情况。

Result: 水印会显著降低医学输出质量，包括词汇损坏、幻觉术语、图像发现错误归因或遗漏；现有聚合指标无法捕捉临床文本中的关键失败模式，掩盖了水印带来的实际退化。

Conclusion: 领域特异性评估是水印模型在医学领域安全部署的前提条件，当前通用基准可能掩盖临床关键的失败，必须建立专门评估标准。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into clinical workflows, stressing the need for reliable traceability of model-generated output with watermarking. Yet, most watermarks are evaluated on general-purpose benchmarks, leaving domains like medicine, where small token-level perturbations can result in significant semantic changes, underexplored. In this work, we present the first rigorous study of how LLM watermarks affect medical performance, benchmarking 5 watermarking schemes across 11 LLMs and 7 VLMs on various tasks spanning unimodal and multimodal clinical reasoning. Importantly, we complement existing evaluations by introducing a human-expert-validated pipeline for systematically auditing medical reasoning quality, terminological precision, and induced hallucinations. Our results reveal that watermarking can induce substantial degradation across multiple failure modes, including lexical corruption, hallucinated terminology, and amplified misattribution or omission of image findings. Notably, we find that the absence of domain-specific analyses, combined with aggregate metrics that miss failures inherent to clinical text, can systematically obscure practical watermark-induced degradations. Our findings establish domain-specific evaluation as a prerequisite for the safe deployment of watermarked models in medicine, where current benchmarks can otherwise mask clinically consequential failures.

</details>


### [174] [ClickGuard: Detecting and Spoiling Clickbait News with Informativeness Measures and Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20463)
*Wojciech Michaluk,Tymoteusz Urban,Mateusz Kubita,Soveatin Kuntur,Anna Wróblewska*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个AI驱动的浏览器扩展，通过混合机器学习架构（Transformer嵌入+语言特征+自定义“诱饵评分”）检测点击诱饵，F1达91%，能在用户访问前后预警并生成文章摘要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 帮助用户避免被互联网上的误导性点击诱饵文章欺骗，提升信息消费质量。

Method: 采用混合机器学习架构，结合Transformer嵌入、语言学特征和自定义“诱饵评分”；评估多种NLP技术后，基于XGBoost构建模型。

Result: 在开放组合数据集上达到91%的F1分数；浏览器扩展可在访问前后预警，给出百分比评分和可解释的指标分析，并提供1-2句的点击诱饵摘要。

Conclusion: 该工具不仅能有效检测点击诱饵，还通过预警、评分解释和摘要增强了用户体验和透明度。

Abstract: This paper presents an AI-driven browser extension that identifies clickbait to help users avoid misleading Internet articles. Moving beyond traditional detection, the application employs a hybrid machine learning architecture that combines transformer-based embeddings with linguistically motivated features and a custom "baitness" score. After evaluating various natural language processing techniques -- from classic vectorizers to large language model (LLM) embeddings -- an XGBoost-based model was developed that achieves an F1-score of 91% on the open combined dataset. Most importantly, the tool can warn users before and after they access a clickbait article. After opening an article, the user receives a percentage score indicating the likelihood that it is clickbait. The prediction is explained based on the analyzed metrics, including those specifically developed within the proposed system. The browser extension also provides a clickbait spoiler -- a one- to two-sentence summary of the entire article. Demo video:https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=IJ1gkQV82C4}{https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=IJ1gkQV82C4

</details>


### [175] [Workload-Aware Caching for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20495)
*Anas Mohamed,Kaizan Haque,Azal Ahmad Khan,Chetan Sharma,Shuwen Ge,Ali Anwar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对多智能体系统的负载感知缓存驱逐策略，通过结合重计算成本、DAG依赖计数和智能体调用频率三种信号，在有限内存下保留最有价值的缓存条目，相比无缓存基线降低最多64.7%的延迟，平均比次优有限容量基线低31.1%的延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有缓存驱逐策略对所有缓存条目一视同仁，仅基于访问历史进行决策，忽略了多智能体执行环境中特有的结构信息和负载信号，导致缓存效率低下。

Method: 提出了一种负载感知的缓存驱逐策略，将重计算成本、DAG依赖计数和智能体调用频率三种信号融合为统一的评分函数，在内存约束下保留最有价值的条目。

Result: 在三个多智能体基准上，相比无缓存基线延迟降低最多64.7%，平均比次优有限容量基线低31.1%的延迟，性能接近无限缓存，准确率持平或超过所有竞争的有限容量方法。

Conclusion: 负载感知缓存是多智能体系统优化中的一种有效且互补的方法，可与计划级缓存和并行智能体执行等技术结合，针对不同效率瓶颈进行优化。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems decompose complex tasks into directed acyclic graphs (DAGs) of specialized agent executions, creating natural opportunities for caching intermediate results across queries. However, existing cache eviction policies treat all cached entries uniformly based on access history, ignoring structural and workload signals uniquely available in agentic execution environments. We present a workload-aware eviction policy that combines three signals, namely recomputation cost, DAG dependency count, and agent invocation frequency, into a unified scoring function that retains the most valuable entries under memory constraints. Evaluated across three multi-agent benchmarks spanning diverse reuse regimes, our policy reduces latency by up to 64.7% relative to the uncached baseline and achieves on average a 31.1% latency reduction over the next best finite-capacity baseline, while approaching the performance of an unbounded cache and maintaining accuracy on par with or exceeding all competing finite-capacity methods. We further show that workload-aware content caching is complementary to other agentic system optimization methods, including plan-level caching and parallel agent execution, with each technique targeting a distinct efficiency bottleneck in multi-agent pipelines.

</details>


### [176] [Stochastic Sampling is Epistemically Shallow: The Dimensionality Gap Between Temperature Variation and Model Diversity in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20464)
*Izhar Ali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文比较了单个大语言模型多次运行（自一致性）与多模型集成在问题间不确定性结构上的差异。自一致性虽能提供准确的问题级不确定性估计，但无法揭示跨问题的相关性结构；而多样化的模型集成则能有效揭示这种结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型重复运行时产生的变异性是否不仅能用于单问题的不确定性估计（自一致性），还能揭示问题之间的相关性结构，类似于集成模型的效果。

Method: 使用Marchenko–Pastur随机矩阵检验方法，比较两种设置：一个模型运行100次（温度τ=1）与24个不同LLM各运行一次（τ=0）。在两个设置中分别分析特征值分布，并将结果与匹配难度的伯努利零模型进行对比。

Result: 在单个模型中，最多只有一个特征值维度高于噪声水平；而在集成模型中，有四个特征值显著高于噪声边界。匹配难度的伯努利零模型在500次蒙特卡洛模拟中最多产生一个虚假信号。

Conclusion: 自一致性方法能提供准确的问题级不确定性估计，但无法检测跨问题间的结构相关性；只有多样化的模型集成才能揭示模型所不知道的知识结构。

Abstract: When a language model gives different answers on repeated runs, does that variation reveal what it does not know? Self-consistency turns the variation into a per-question uncertainty estimate via majority voting. But does the same variation reveal cross-question structure -- related questions flipping together, the way a diverse ensemble does? We compare two regimes on the same questions: one model run $100$ times at $τ=1$ versus an ensemble of $24$ LLMs run once each at $τ=0$. A Marchenko--Pastur random-matrix test separates signal from sampling noise on both sides. Within any single model, at most one dimension rises above noise across five families and three benchmarks (MMLU, HellaSwag, GSM8K). Across the ensemble, four eigenvalues clear the noise edge, while a matched-difficulty Bernoulli null produces at most one in $500$ Monte Carlo draws. Self-consistency gives accurate per-question uncertainty but no detectable cross-question structure; only a diverse ensemble surfaces what a model does not know.

</details>


### [177] [MKEvolve: A Modular Multi-Agent Framework for Kernel Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20501)
*Jason Yoo,Rajarshi Saha,Shaowei Zhu,Tao Yu,Wei Tang,Youngsuk Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MKEvolve通过迭代式模块分解与LLM驱动的子内核优化，实现了高性能可配置内核的自动生成，在KernelBench L2/L3上比直接合成方法在正确性和加速比上均有提升，同时降低35%的token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM生成的代码虽然进步显著，但在硬件加速器上编写正确且高性能的内核仍然是扩展现代ML工作负载的关键瓶颈。

Method: 提出MKEvolve框架，通过迭代式模块分解（拆分与融合）协同进化复杂PyTorch模块及其LLM生成的子内核，利用LLM驱动的束搜索独立优化每个子内核。

Result: 在KernelBench L2和L3上，MKEvolve相比端到端直接合成基线，在正确性和加速比上均有提升，并减少了高达35%的LLM token使用量。

Conclusion: 模块化分解策略使得生成的内核具有可配置性、可解释性和可迁移性，为高性能内核生成提供了一种更高效、更可控的范式。

Abstract: Despite rapid progress in LLM-based code generation, writing correct and performant kernels for hardware accelerators remains a key bottleneck in scaling modern ML workloads. We present MKEvolve (Modular Kernel Evolve), a framework that iteratively co-evolves a modular decomposition of complex PyTorch modules and the LLM-generated kernel for each submodule, refining the decomposition by splitting and fusing across iterations while independently improving each subkernel via LLM-driven beam search. The resulting kernels are programmatic compositions of independently verified subkernels, making them configurable (subkernel implementations are swappable), interpretable (errors and speedups are traceable to specific subkernels), and readily adaptable to related model architectures. Experiments with Triton on KernelBench L2 and L3, spanning multi-operator sequences and full model architectures, show that MKEvolve improves both correctness and speedup over end-to-end direct synthesis baselines while reducing LLM token usage by up to 35%.

</details>


### [178] [JAXBench: Benchmarking Autonomous TPU Kernel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20466)
*Arya Tschand,Charles Hong,Julian Walker,Nina Cai,Shangkun Wang,Suvinay Subramanian,Sundar Dev,Vijay Janapa Reddi,Amir Yazdanbakhsh,Sethu Sankaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: JAXBench是一个面向TPU的原生基准测试套件，包含50个JAX工作负载，用于评估AI生成的GPU/TPU内核优化。研究发现，针对特定目标的上文（TPU文档）比模型规模更重要，结合搜索结构的Autocomp方法能达到1.36x的几何平均加速比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU领域已有成熟的基准测试推动内核优化，但TPU缺乏类似的标准化基准。作者旨在填补这一空白，为TPU上的AI生成内核优化建立共享评测平台。

Method: 构建包含50个JAX工作负载的基准套件（17个来自MaxText的ML算子+33个从KernelBench翻译验证的算子），并对比四种反馈驱动方法在生成Pallas内核上的表现，评估文档上下文和搜索结构的影响。

Result: 目标特定上下文比模型规模更重要，TPU文档条件化将正确率从5.8%提升至37.3%；Autocomp的束搜索达到1.36x加速比，在8个手调内核上达到1.60x（接近Tokamax的2.08x上界）。

Conclusion: 高质量TPU内核优化仍然具有挑战性，但文档条件和搜索结构能显著提升AI生成内核的性能。JAXBench将开源以支持社区贡献。

Abstract: Rigorous benchmarks have driven progress in autonomous GPU kernel performance optimization by establishing a shared target to hillclimb on, but no equivalent exists for TPUs. We present JAXBench, a TPU-native benchmark suite for AI-generated kernel optimization on Google Cloud TPUs. JAXBench comprises 50 JAX workloads that are both relevant and provide headroom for optimization. We extract 17 production ML operators from architectures in the public MaxText library such as Llama-3.1, DeepSeek-V3, Mixtral, Mamba-2, and AlphaFold2, and translate 33 operators from KernelBench that are validated for correctness and set with new problem sizes that achieve high TPU v6e MXU utilization. Eight of the 17 production operators ship with hand-optimized Pallas kernels from the public Tokamax library and block-size tuned to establish an expert upper-bound baseline. We evaluate four feedback-driven methods on generating candidate Pallas kernels for JAXBench. Across the full suite with Gemini 3 Flash, we find that target-specific context matters more than model scale on a sparsely-documented DSL like Pallas. Conditioning on curated TPU documentation raises per-sample correctness from 5.8% to 37.3% and solves 48 of 50 benchmarks at a 1.28x geomean speedup. Search structure yields significant gains once correctness is achieved, with Autocomp's beam-search pipeline reaching a 1.36x geomean speedup over XLA. On the 8 hand-tuned kernels, Autocomp reaches 1.60x geomean over XLA, recovering most of the 2.08x Tokamax upper bound but trailing on the specialized paged and ragged attention operators. High-quality TPU kernel optimization remains a challenging task, and we release the JAXBench benchmark, evaluation harness, and baseline results to support open source contributions.

</details>


### [179] [AppWorld-UL: Benchmarking Diverse Agent-User Interactions for Tool-Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20536)
*Junzhi Chen,Harsh Trivedi,Jane Pan,Michael JQ Zhang,Tejas Srinivasan,Niranjan Balasubramanian,Ashish Sabharwal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AppWorld-UL，一个包含516个需要智能体与用户交互的日常数字任务基准。该基准基于9个模拟应用，通过引入歧义和约束来强制要求人机交互。评估显示顶级模型Claude Opus 4.7成功率仅48.6%，组合任务低至35.7%，表明该基准具有挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准无法捕捉智能体与用户交互的多样性，且局限于小型、无状态变更的API环境，无法反映真实世界中人机协作的复杂性。作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 基于AppWorld框架（含9个模拟应用如Amazon、Spotify），系统性地修改原始任务以引入歧义和约束，迫使智能体需要用户交互。使用精心设计的知识边界提示LLM模拟用户行为，提供比现有方法更可靠的模拟。

Result: Claude Opus 4.7在AppWorld-UL上成功率48.6%；较难的组合子集仅35.7%；场景级严格指标下组合任务仅21.3%。分析表明正确的用户交互对任务成功至关重要。

Conclusion: AppWorld-UL是一个高难度的基准，能够有效推动用户参与的智能体交互研究，揭示了当前模型在真实人机协作场景中的显著不足。

Abstract: Tool-use agents that address day-to-day digital tasks such as ordering groceries must not only operate applications, but also interact with the user, e.g., to ask clarification questions, prompt for confirmation, and inform the user when the instruction is infeasible. However, current benchmarks for evaluating agent-user interactions do not capture the diversity of such interactions. Further, they operate in small environments with few, often non-state-changing, APIs. To address this gap, we introduce AppWorld-UL, a ``user-in-the-loop'' benchmark of 516 challenging tasks requiring diverse agent-user interactions. Building upon the AppWorld framework with 9 popular simulated apps like Amazon and Spotify, we systematically modify original tasks to introduce ambiguities and constraints that necessitate various types of agent-user interaction. User behavior is simulated by an LLM prompted to respond with carefully designed knowledge boundaries, offering more reliable simulation than the unconstrained or overly rigid alternatives used in prior work. Our evaluation reveals that a state-of-the-art LLM, Claude Opus 4.7, achieves only 48.6% success on AppWorld-UL, and only 35.7% on the harder, compositional subset. On the stricter, scenario-level metric, compositional task performance drops to only 21.3%. Our analysis reveals that correct user-interaction is crucial for success. This demonstrates the benchmark's difficulty and its potential to advance research on user-in-the-loop tool-use agents.

</details>


### [180] [DC-Leap: Training-Free Acceleration of dLLMs via Draft-Guided Contiguous Leaping Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20467)
*Yanhua Jiao,Tianyi Wu,Xiaoxi Sun,Yulin Li,HuiLing Zhen,Libo Qin,Baotian Hu,Zhuotao Tian,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DC-Leap通过动态连续验证策略消除联合概率依赖误差，在适度置信度区间实现扩散大语言模型最高105.02倍加速，且无需额外训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散LLM的并行解码策略因联合概率依赖误差(JPDE)被迫采用过于保守的置信度阈值，导致去噪迭代冗余、推理速度次优。

Method: 提出DC-Leap框架：(1) 动态连续验证(DCV)策略，将严格有序的因果约束融入并行解码，逐步验证token依赖以消除JPDE；(2) 草稿引导解码机制，让草稿token向前跳跃扩展上下文，保留双向注意力的结构优势。

Result: 标准基准测试中，MBPP长序列生成加速达53.19倍，结合KV-Cache最高加速105.02倍，且生成质量与原始模型相当。

Conclusion: DC-Leap在无需训练的条件下，通过解决JPDE问题实现了扩散LLM的可靠加速，尤其适合长序列生成场景。

Abstract: While parallel decoding is central to the efficiency of Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs), current strategies are often hindered by overly conservative confidence thresholds. These thresholds, necessitated by the Joint Probability Dependence Error (JPDE), result in redundant denoising iterations and suboptimal inference speeds. To overcome this, we propose DC-Leap, a training-free framework that enables reliable acceleration of dLLMs in the moderate-confidence regime. DC-Leap introduces a Dynamic Contiguous Verification strategy that integrates strictly-ordered causal constraints into the parallel decoding process. By progressively validating token dependencies, this mechanism effectively neutralizes the JPDE, enabling reliable acceleration with comparable performance. Furthermore, DC-Leap incorporates the draft-guided decoding mechanism, where the draft helps extend the context by leaping forward across multiple tokens, providing look-ahead context and retaining the structural benefits of bidirectional attention during inference. Extensive experiments on standard benchmarks demonstrate that DC-Leap achieves substantial speedups, up to 53.19x on MBPP for long-sequence generation, and up to 105.02x when combined with KV-Cache with comparable generation quality. Code is available at https://github.com/ffh-wyls/DC-Leap .

</details>


### [181] [InferenceBench: A Benchmark for Open-Ended LLM Inference Optimization by AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20468)
*Jehyeok Yeon,Ben Rank,Maksym Andriushchenko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: InferenceBench是一个评估AI agent在开放工程环境中优化LLM推理速度的基准，发现agent虽然具备领域知识但缺乏系统探索多样配置的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多评估agent在预设工作流或狭窄行动空间上的表现，即使是开放任务也可通过记忆已知解决方案完成，难以区分真正的优化与记忆。需要一个需要真实工程探索而非记忆的评估环境。

Method: 设计了InferenceBench基准，要求agent在2小时内将LLM部署到推理服务器并优化速度。包含4个场景：预填充延迟、解码延迟、并发吞吐量、三者平衡。评估15种前沿agent配置。

Result: Agent比朴素PyTorch基线提升8.08倍，匹配或超过vLLM默认设置（4.05倍），但低于同等时间预算下的简单超参数搜索（11.53倍）。Agent趋向于集中在一个推理框架，测试少量配置，剩余时间用于重复测量而非探索。

Conclusion: Agent的瓶颈不在于领域知识，而在于提出多样配置、系统评估和提交最优解的能力。InferenceBench反映了agent在开放工程环境中记忆方案只能带来有限提升。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly used to automate research and development tasks, yet existing benchmarks typically evaluate them on prescribed workflows or narrow action spaces. Even nominally open-ended tasks can often be solved by retrieving a well-known recipe and tuning a few hyperparameters, making it unclear whether strong results reflect genuine optimization or memorized solutions. We introduce InferenceBench, where an agent must deploy an OpenAI-compatible inference server and optimize the speed of LLM inference. Each agent receives a target LLM, one H100 GPU, an optimization scenario, and a wall-clock time budget of two hours. Three optimization scenarios isolate distinct bottlenecks of inference (prefill latency, decode latency, and concurrent request throughput) and a fourth balances all three at the same time. Across 15 frontier agent configurations, agents reliably improve over a naive PyTorch baseline (up to $8.08\times$) and often match or exceed serving engines with default settings ($4.05\times$ for vLLM), but still fall below a simple hyperparameter search under the same time budget (up to $11.53\times$). Qualitative analysis of agent trajectories shows that although agents enumerate many relevant optimization techniques, they overwhelmingly converge on a single inference framework. They test only a few distinct configurations and spend the remaining budget re-measuring, repairing, or optimizing hyperparameters rather than exploring substantially different strategies. This suggests the bottleneck is not domain knowledge, but the ability to propose diverse configurations, evaluate them systematically, and submit the best identified solution. Overall, InferenceBench reflects the ability of agents to operate in an open-ended AI engineering setting, where memorized solutions lead to limited improvements.

</details>


### [182] [HiMe: Real-Time Self-Hosted Personal Agent Platform for Health Insights with Wearable Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21019)
*Wei Liu,Siya Qi,Linhai Zhang,Lorainne Tudor Car,Yulan He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HiMe是一个本地可部署、隐私优先的智能体平台，能够实时处理来自多种可穿戴设备的健康数据，实现个性化健康监测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统可穿戴健康信号分析方法受限于僵化的分析框架和有限的个性化能力，且缺乏开源、本地可部署、能实时处理个人健康数据同时保护隐私的平台。

Method: HiMe遵循三个设计原则：1）将数据库作为一等组件；2）联合优化有效性和效率以实现低成本的帕累托最优平衡；3）实时处理数据同时长期建模用户状态。

Result: 构建了一个完全兼容多种可穿戴设备实时健康数据生态系统的、本地可部署的隐私优先智能体平台。

Conclusion: HiMe使个人能够利用个人健康智能体实现持续、个性化的健康监测，从而改善健康福祉。

Abstract: Traditional approaches to wearable health signal analysis, such as smartwatches, are constrained by rigid analytical frameworks and limited personalisation. The emergence of LLM agents creates a new opportunity for Personal Health Agentic Analysis, where health insights can be generated adaptively and in context. However, currently there is no open-source locally deployable platform capable of processing personal health data in real time while preserving privacy. We present HiMe, a locally deployable, privacy-first agent platform that is fully compatible with real-time health data ecosystems across a wide range of wearable devices. HiMe is guided by three design principles. The database is treated as a first-class component. Effectiveness and efficiency are jointly optimised to achieve a low-cost Pareto-optimal balance. Data are processed in real time while the user is modelled over the long term. Together, these principles make it practical for individuals to harness Personal Health Agents for continuous, personalised health monitoring for better wellbeing.

</details>


### [183] [DecodeShare: Tracing the Shared Subspace of LLM Decode-Time Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20469)
*Zishan Shao,Lixun Zhang,Kangning Cui,Yixiao Wang,Ting Jiang,Hancheng Ye,Qinsi Wang,Zhixu Du,Yuzhe Fu,Fan Yang,Danyang Zhuo,Yiran Chen,Hai Helen Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DecodeShare协议，发现LLMs在解码阶段的隐藏状态中存在跨任务的共享低维子空间，移除该子空间会显著损害决策性能，且该子空间对激活引导有重要影响，可作为解码时的高效因果通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解LLMs在KV缓存推理的解码阶段是否使用跨任务通用结构，以及这种结构的作用机制和因果影响。

Method: 提出DecodeShare协议，识别解码阶段隐藏状态中跨任务共享的低维子空间，并通过干扰该子空间与预填充或随机子空间进行对比实验，验证其因果作用。同时分析该子空间对激活引导的影响。

Result: 解码共享子空间被干扰时，决策性能下降远大于干扰预填充或随机子空间；解码共享子空间与激活引导方向重叠，去除后能分离功能角色；解码时的引导向量比预填充代理更可靠。

Conclusion: 解码阶段存在紧凑但高效的跨任务共享因果通道，对实践中的激活引导和模型部署具有重要意义。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) handle many tasks with one set of parameters, but under KV-cached inference it is unclear what task-general structure, if any, is used at decode time rather than during prefill. We propose DecodeShare, a protocol that identifies a low-dimensional subspace consistently shared across tasks in decode-time hidden states, and then tests its causal role by removing that subspace only during decoding. In our experiments, disturbing the discovered shared subspace degrades decision performance far more than disturbing either a prefill-derived or random subspace under the same intervention budget. We further show this decode-shared subspace has practical consequences for activation steering: common steering directions can overlap the task-general decode channel. Projecting out this shared subspace directly separates the functional roles of the two components, while evaluating steering vectors at decode-time yields more reliable signal for downstream deployment than prefill-based proxies. Despite its compactness, the shared subspace can serve as a high-leverage causal channel at decode time. Code is available at: https://github.com/Zishan-Shao/decodeshare.git.

</details>


### [184] [SafeStep: AI-powered Travel Assistance for Elderly People with Frailty or Dementia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21156)
*Elderly People with Frailty or Dementia Azul Debenedetti,David Gamez,Franco Such,Nik Kairinos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SafeStep是一个AI驱动的出行辅助系统，通过新颖的旅行图表示结合路线规划与预测建模，为老年人及认知障碍患者生成个性化干预方案，并在26次真实出行中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 英国超过100万人患有虚弱或痴呆症，这些人群在城市环境中的出行能力严重受损，亟需智能辅助系统来提升他们的出行安全性和自信心。

Method: 核心创新是旅行图（Travel Graph）表示，将路线规划与预测建模结合。系统为每个出行阶段：(i) 利用LLM和Anticip8行为预测引擎生成个性化故障场景；(ii) 提出针对性干预措施；(iii) 估计干预对结果概率的影响，从而选择最大化到达目的地的干预方案。

Result: 实验表明，Anticip8故障预测与GPT干预评估的组合效果最佳。26次真实出行用户反馈显示，SafeStep提升了出行自信和感知安全性，但界面可用性需针对目标人群改进。

Conclusion: SafeStep在辅助老年人出行方面显示出潜力，未来将改进并发布该系统。其AI框架可推广至心理健康、职业指导和成瘾治疗等其他领域。

Abstract: More than a million people in the UK suffer from frailty or dementia, which severely compromise their ability to travel in urban environments. This paper presents SafeStep, an AI-driven travel system that assists elderly users with their journeys. At the core of SafeStep is a novel travel graph representation, which integrates route planning with predictive modelling. For each stage of a journey, the system (i) generates personalized failure scenarios using a combi-nation of LLMs and the Anticip8 behavioral prediction engine, (ii) proposes targeted interventions, and (iii) estimates the impact of interventions on out-come probabilities. This enables SafeStep to select interventions that maximize the likelihood of the person reaching their destination. SafeStep was evaluated through experiments on travel graph generation and a field study involving 26 real-world journeys. Results showed that combining Anticip8 for failure pre-diction with GPT-based models for intervention evaluation yields the most re-liable performance. User feedback indicated that SafeStep improves confidence and perceived safety during travel, although interface usability needs to be im-proved for the target demographic. In the future, we would like to improve and release SafeStep. The AI system that was developed for SafeStep could be ap-plied in other areas, such as mental health, career coaching and addiction treatment.

</details>


### [185] [PlanE: Meta Planning of Data, Tuning, and Inference for Extractive-based LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20470)
*Jiacheng Wang,Weiyan Zhang,Guangya Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PlanE是一个面向抽取式LLM的规划框架，包含数据分解、指令微调和提示推理，并引入DTI规划器为特定数据集选择最优基座模型及其组合，实验证明其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM任务微调需要大量指令数据集，标注成本高且缺乏针对特定任务的优化方法。

Method: 提出PlanE框架，包含数据分解、指令微调、提示推理三部分；并引入Data-Tuning-Inference (DTI)规划器，为特定数据集选择最优基座LLM及其最佳组合。

Result: 在两个维度上验证有效性：(1)同一基座LLM在不同数据集上的表现；(2)同一数据集使用不同基座LLM的表现。DTI规划器在不同优化目标下也展现出泛化能力。

Conclusion: PlanE框架能有效降低标注成本并提升LLM在特定任务上的构建效率，DTI规划器具有良好的泛化性。

Abstract: Enhancing the task-specific capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) primarily requires substantial instruction-tuning datasets. However, the sheer volume of such data imposes a considerable annotation cost, and a lack of optimization methods for tailoring LLMs to specific tasks.
